# Introduction | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/introduction.md)
.

DOKU (**PT Nusa Satu Inti Artha**), founded in 2007, is Indonesia’s first locally owned electronic payment solutions provider. As a pioneer in the payment gateway industry, DOKU has played a key role in offering secure, reliable, and locally tailored payment solutions that address the evolving needs of online merchants. Since 2021, DOKU has expanded its presence to Malaysia, with **SimplePay Gateway Sdn. Bhd.** becoming part of the DOKU Group. This milestone strengthened DOKU’s regional footprint and enabled the company to deliver seamless, reliable, and locally tailored payment solutions to businesses across Southeast Asia.

# Payment Link: Dynamic Currency Conversion | Product Updates | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/august-2026/payment-link-dynamic-currency-conversion.md)
.

DCC (Dynamic Currency Conversion) is now available on **DOKU Payment Link**.

GitBook Assistant

Merchants can enable DCC directly when creating a Payment Link by turning on the **“Use DCC”** toggle. Once enabled, customers can view the estimated payment amount in a familiar currency, while the actual payment is still processed using the original currency of the Payment Link.

GitBook Assistant

**Supported currencies:** USD, SGD, MYR, JPY, AUD, RUB, and RMB.

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252FSD9VX1CNiVZZvZtEcTkY%252FMonthly%2520Product%2520August_%2523%2520KV7%2520%281%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Def1b1bc3-b460-4b46-9013-2f0caead7255&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9380ad96e937905047ed88c4a9efea1d&sv=3)

* * *

### 🔍 What’s New?[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#whats-new)

DCC adds a currency conversion view to Payment Link, allowing customers to check the estimated value of their payment in another currency.

GitBook Assistant

For example, a Payment Link priced at **IDR 1,000,000** can be displayed as an **estimated value** in **USD, SGD, MYR, JPY, AUD, RUB, or RMB** based on the customer's preference.

GitBook Assistant

The selected currency is only used to **display the estimated amount**. It does not change the original transaction currency.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 💡 Why It Matters[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#why-it-matters)

DCC helps international customers better understand the value of a Payment Link before making a payment.

GitBook Assistant

It provides a more familiar price reference without changing the actual payment currency.

GitBook Assistant

#### **For Merchants**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#for-merchants)

*   Make Payment Links easier to understand for international customers
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Improve the payment experience for customers from different countries
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Support businesses that serve international customers without changing their existing payment currency
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

#### **For Customers**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#for-customers)

*   See an estimated price in a familiar currency
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Understand the payment amount more easily
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Make payment decisions with better price visibility
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### 🚀 How It Works[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#how-it-works)

#### 🔄 Before[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#before)

Without DCC, customers see the Payment Link amount only in the original transaction currency.

GitBook Assistant

For example:

GitBook Assistant

> **IDR 2,000,000**
> 
> GitBook Assistant

International customers need to estimate the value themselves in their preferred currency.

GitBook Assistant

#### 🆕 After[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#after)

With DCC enabled, customers can view an estimated value in another supported currency.

GitBook Assistant

For example:

GitBook Assistant

> **IDR 2,000,000** ≈ **SGD 143.59**
> 
> GitBook Assistant

Customers can switch between supported currencies to better understand the payment amount.

GitBook Assistant

**The actual payment remains in IDR.**

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### ⚙️ How to Enable[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#how-to-enable)

Enabling DCC is simple:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Create a **Payment Link**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Turn on the **“Use DCC”** toggle.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Create and share the Payment Link with your customer.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Customers can select a supported currency to view the estimated payment amount.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Payment is completed using the **original Payment Link currency**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### 🎯 **Potential Use Cases**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#potential-use-cases)

DCC is particularly useful for merchants that serve international customers, such as:

GitBook Assistant

*   **Hotels & Hospitality** — international guests can easily understand room or booking prices.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Tourism & Travel** — help overseas customers understand tour, ticket, or travel-related payments.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Retail & E-commerce** — make product prices easier to understand for international buyers.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Education & Events** — support international participants paying through Payment Link.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Professional Services** — provide clearer pricing for overseas customers.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### ⚠️ Important Notes[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog#important-notes)

DCC **does not mean the merchant receives payment in USD, SGD, JPY, or other supported currencies**.

GitBook Assistant

The selected currency is only used to show the customer an estimated equivalent value.

GitBook Assistant

> **DCC = Currency visibility, not currency conversion of the actual payment.**
> 
> GitBook Assistant

* * *

For assistance, contact our Care Team at care@doku.com or (021) 1500963.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Thank you and best regards, **DOKU Product Team**

GitBook Assistant

[NextASIA Chatbot](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot)

# ASIA Chatbot | Product Updates | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot.md)
.

**ASIA** is now more than a chatbot that answers merchant questions. With the latest update, ASIA can **help merchants check information and complete selected tasks directly from chat**, making it easier to get things done and saving time on navigating the DOKU Dashboard.

GitBook Assistant

**ASIA is your Dashboard assistant** helping answer your needs without having to read through documentation or submit a ticket to DOKU Care for every request.

GitBook Assistant

The update includes **transaction insights, settlement checks, payment method activation, automatic Care escalation, and faster responses**, giving merchants a simpler and more efficient way to manage common payment-related tasks.

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252FLAu2UPpLxzMlgQnpezW5%252FMonthly%2520Product%2520August_%2523%2520KV5%2520%281%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc5ba19fa-e6ec-48e2-ae7e-89e36fad7de5&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=d76437968adea4a319766b7127420b3f&sv=3)

* * *

### 🔍 What’s New?[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#whats-new)

#### **1\. Transaction Assistance**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#id-1.-transaction-assistance)

Merchants can ask ASIA to help with transaction-related information, including:

GitBook Assistant

*   Transaction details and payment status
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Payment journey
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   GMV calculation
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Payment method usage
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

For example:

GitBook Assistant

> “What payment method was used the most in the last 7 days?”
> 
> GitBook Assistant

[**Also available on DOKU Agentic Payment**](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/doku-mcp-server)

GitBook Assistant

#### **2\. Settlement Assistance**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#id-2.-settlement-assistance)

ASIA can help merchants check:

GitBook Assistant

*   Settlement status
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Whether a settlement is pending or completed
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Settlement date
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

Merchants can also ask ASIA to check the settlement status of specific transactions.

GitBook Assistant

[**Also available on DOKU Agentic Payment**](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/doku-mcp-server)

GitBook Assistant

#### **3\. Payment Method Activation**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#id-3.-payment-method-activation)

Merchants can activate available payment methods directly through chat.

GitBook Assistant

For example:

GitBook Assistant

> “I want to activate QRIS.”
> 
> GitBook Assistant

Instead of navigating to **Settings → Service**, merchants can simply ask ASIA to help with the activation.

GitBook Assistant

#### **4\. Automatic Care Escalation**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#id-4.-automatic-care-escalation)

If ASIA cannot resolve a merchant's issue, it can automatically create a **Freshdesk ticket** and escalate the case to the **Care team**.

GitBook Assistant

This means merchants no longer need to manually submit a form or send an email to create a support ticket.

GitBook Assistant

#### **5\. Smarter & Faster Responses**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#id-5.-smarter-and-faster-responses)

ASIA's knowledge base has been updated with the latest product and operational information, improving response accuracy.

GitBook Assistant

Average response time has also improved from approximately **9 seconds to 5–6 seconds**.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 💡 Why It Matters[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#why-it-matters)

Previously, merchants needed to navigate different Dashboard menus or contact the Care team for certain requests.

GitBook Assistant

With the latest ASIA update, merchants can get information and complete selected tasks **through one conversational interface**, making common interactions faster and easier. with key benefits:

GitBook Assistant

*   **Less navigation:** get help directly from chat
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Faster information access:** quickly check transactions and settlements
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Simpler activation:** activate available payment methods through chat
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Seamless support:** unresolved issues can be automatically escalated to Care
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Faster responses:** improved knowledge base and response time
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### 🚀 How It Works[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#how-it-works)

**ASIA uses MCP Server to connect its conversational experience with selected DOKU capabilities, including Transaction and Settlement.** This enables merchants to retrieve relevant transaction and settlement information directly through their conversations with ASIA. This allows ASIA to go beyond answering questions and **retrieve information or perform selected actions based on the merchant's request.**

GitBook Assistant

#### 🔄 Before[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#before)

Merchants typically need to:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Open ASIA the DOKU Dashboard.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Using chatbot for only general questions.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  If ask for action realted, navigate to the relevant menu.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Search for transaction or settlement information.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Go to **Settings → Service** to manage payment methods.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
6.  Contact Care manually if they need further assistance.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

#### 🆕 After[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#after)

Merchants can simply:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Open **ASIA** from the DOKU Dashboard.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Ask a question or request an action.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  ASIA retrieves the relevant information or performs the available action.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  If ASIA cannot resolve the issue, a **Freshdesk ticket** is automatically created and escalated to Care.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### 💼 **Key Features**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#key-features)

*   Transaction details and payment status
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Payment journey insights
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   GMV calculation
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Payment method usage insights
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Settlement status and settlement date
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Payment method activation through chat
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Automatic Freshdesk ticket creation
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Automatic escalation to the Care team
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Updated knowledge base
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Faster average response time of **5–6 seconds**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### **📌 Key Details**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#key-details)

*   Payment method activation is available for payment methods that can be activated through ASIA.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   If ASIA cannot resolve an issue, the conversation can be escalated to the Care team through Freshdesk.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   ASIA is available on the **DOKU Dashboard** across supported environments.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### ⚠️ Important Notes[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot#important-notes)

ASIA's capabilities will continue to expand. Additional tools and actions may be introduced in future updates, including potential support for other operational requests.

GitBook Assistant

For now, merchants should use ASIA for supported transaction, settlement, payment method, and support-related requests.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

For assistance, contact our Care Team at care@doku.com or (021) 1500963.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Thank you and best regards, **DOKU Product Team**

# Refund Services - Whatsapp Channel | Product Updates | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel.md)
.

Refund notification and passcode now supports delivery through WhatsApp via the official DOKU Business Account, providing a more secure and reliable way for customers to receive refund notifications.

GitBook Assistant

This update replaces the previous SMS-based notification flow while maintaining the existing refund process for merchants.

GitBook Assistant

[**Learn about Refund Services**](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/refund-service)

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252FnUrHngl6bgnVomW10ELU%252FMonthly%2520Product%2520Update%2520July_5%2520%281%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Deb9b46ff-7aa6-4786-b59a-19d1f20609ae&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=110881c95e5f4982592960f314110240&sv=3)

* * *

### 🔍 What’s New?[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#whats-new)

Refund notifications are now delivered through **WhatsApp** instead of SMS for merchants who enable the feature.

GitBook Assistant

Customers will receive their refund information directly through the official DOKU WhatsApp account, including the refund link, verification passcode, expiration information, and action button where applicable.

GitBook Assistant

Depending on the refund type, customers will receive one of two notification formats:

GitBook Assistant

*   **Refund Link** – includes a refund link, verification passcode, expiry information, and **"Receive Refund"** call-to-action.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Direct Refund** – includes refund confirmation and a **"Check Status"** button without requiring a passcode.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### 💡 Why It Matters[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#why-it-matters)

SMS has long been used as the primary notification channel for Refund Link. However, SMS delivery introduces security risks, including the possibility of unauthorized access to refund links or delayed message delivery.

GitBook Assistant

By moving notifications to WhatsApp, DOKU provides a more trusted communication channel while strengthening refund security through passcode verification and official business messaging.

GitBook Assistant

This enhancement helps merchants improve customer confidence throughout the refund process while reducing potential fraud risks.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### **🚀 Updated Experiences**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#updated-experiences)

#### 🔄 Before[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#before)

Previously, customers received Refund Link notifications via SMS.

GitBook Assistant

Although functional, SMS delivery presented several challenges:

GitBook Assistant

*   Refund notifications could be delayed or fail to reach customers.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   SMS messages were more vulnerable to interception by unauthorized parties.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Customers relied on SMS to access refund links, creating potential security risks during the refund process.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

#### 🆕 After[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#after)

Beginning August 10th, 2026, all refund communications will default to WhatsApp and/or email if applicable Refund notifications delivered through the **official DOKU WhatsApp Business Account**.

GitBook Assistant

Depending on the refund type, customers will receive either:

GitBook Assistant

*   **Refund Link** — including a secure refund link, verification passcode, expiry information, and a **"Receive Refund"** button.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Direct Refund** — including a refund confirmation message and a **"Check Status"** button without requiring a passcode.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

This provides a more secure, reliable, and familiar refund experience while helping reduce the risk of unauthorized refund access.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 📌 Refund Methods[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#refund-methods)

**Non-Direct Refund**

GitBook Assistant

They will open the link and enter their own bank account details before the refund is processed. **Best for:** Merchants who do not have the customer's bank account information.

GitBook Assistant

**Direct Refund**

GitBook Assistant

The customer receives a WhatsApp notification confirming the refund, without needing to enter any additional information. **Best for:** Merchants who already have the customer's bank account information.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### ⚙️ How It Works[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#how-it-works)

*   **Enable** WhatsApp Notification for your merchant account.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Continue sending refund requests using the existing **customer\_phone** parameter — no API changes are required.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   DOKU automatically delivers the refund notification through the official WhatsApp Business Account.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Customers receive the appropriate notification based on the refund type:
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
    *   **Refund Link** with passcode verification, or
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    *   **Direct Refund** with refund status confirmation.
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    
*   Customers complete the refund process securely through the provided WhatsApp message.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### **💼 Key Feature**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#key-feature)

*   Deliver Refund Link notifications through the official DOKU WhatsApp Business Account.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Support dedicated notification formats for Refund Link and Direct Refund.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Include passcode verification for Refund Link transactions.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Improve delivery reliability compared to SMS notifications.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Strengthen refund security by reducing the risk of unauthorized refund access.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   No API changes required for existing Refund Link integrations.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### **📌 Key Details**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel#key-details)

*   No API parameter changes are required. Merchants continue using the existing **customer\_phone** field.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   WhatsApp notification must be **enabled by the merchant** and is not activated by default.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Once WhatsApp notification is activated, SMS notification will be automatically disabled for that merchant.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Passcode verification is only applicable to **Refund Link** transactions. Direct Refund notifications do not require a passcode.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

For assistance, contact our Care Team at care@doku.com or (021) 1500963.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Thank you and best regards, **DOKU Product Team**

GitBook Assistant

# New Payment Method - VA Bank BJB & Bank Sinarmas | Product Updates | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas.md)
.

DOKU is expanding its Virtual Account (VA) payment methods with the addition of **Bank BJB** and **Bank Sinarmas**, giving merchants more payment options while helping customers pay through their preferred banks.

GitBook Assistant

With this update, merchants can easily activate the new payment methods through the DOKU Dashboard and immediately offer broader payment coverage to their customers.

GitBook Assistant

[**Learn How to Activate It**](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/manage-payment-methods)

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252FWR7YABDa6xmjcibpy8Lt%252FMonthly%2520Product%2520Update%2520July_7.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Ddd292d71-37f0-40b7-bd88-5cfd7db09e9b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=22f385c88165ca24a91a17950f6d9b8a&sv=3)

* * *

### 🔍 What’s New?[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas#whats-new)

Two new Virtual Account payment methods are now available on DOKU:

GitBook Assistant

**Bank Sinarmas Virtual Account**

GitBook Assistant

Available for merchants using DOKU Checkout, including Payment Link and Digital Catalog. Once activated, customers will automatically see Bank Sinarmas as one of the available payment options during checkout.

GitBook Assistant

**Bank BJB Virtual Account**

GitBook Assistant

Available for merchants using Direct API integration, allowing businesses with their own payment interface to offer Bank BJB Virtual Accounts to customers.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 💡 Why It Matters[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas#why-it-matters)

Every business serves different customer segments, and payment preferences often vary by region and banking ecosystem.

GitBook Assistant

By adding Bank BJB and Bank Sinarmas Virtual Accounts, merchants can provide customers with more convenient payment options while reducing the likelihood of abandoned payments caused by limited banking choices or interbank transfer fees.

GitBook Assistant

This expansion also helps businesses increase payment accessibility, particularly for customers in **West Java and Banten** through Bank BJB and customers within the broader **Sinarmas ecosystem**.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### **🚀 Updated Experiences**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas#updated-experiences)

**🔄 Before**

GitBook Assistant

Previously, merchants could only offer the existing Virtual Account payment methods supported by DOKU.

GitBook Assistant

Customers banking with Bank BJB or Bank Sinarmas often needed to transfer through other banks, potentially incurring additional transfer fees or choosing alternative payment methods.

GitBook Assistant

**🆕 After**

GitBook Assistant

Merchants can now activate Bank BJB and Bank Sinarmas Virtual Accounts directly from the DOKU Dashboard.

GitBook Assistant

Once activated:

GitBook Assistant

*   Bank Sinarmas automatically appears on supported DOKU Checkout experiences.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Direct API merchants can begin accepting payments through Bank BJB.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Customers enjoy more payment choices using their preferred banking services.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### ⚙️ How It Works[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas#how-it-works)

Activating the new Virtual Account channels only takes a few steps:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Log in to the **DOKU Dashboard**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Navigate to **Settings**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Select **Services**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Click **Add Services**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Under **Bank Transfer**, select:
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
    *   **Virtual Account Bank BJB**
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    *   **Virtual Account Bank Sinarmas**
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    
6.  Save your changes to activate the selected payment methods.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

Once activated, the new payment methods will become available based on your integration type.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### **💼 Key Feature**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas#key-feature)

With this update, merchants can:

GitBook Assistant

*   Accept payments through **Bank BJB Virtual Account**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Accept payments through **Bank Sinarmas Virtual Account**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Enable new Virtual Account channels directly from the DOKU Dashboard.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Expand payment coverage across more Indonesian banking customers.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Increase payment convenience without changing existing payment workflows.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### 📌 Things to Know[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas#things-to-know)

With this update, merchants can:

GitBook Assistant

*   **Bank Sinarmas Virtual Account** is available for merchants using **DOKU Checkout**, including Payment Link and Digital Catalog.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Bank BJB Virtual Account** is available for merchants using **Direct API** integration.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Activation is required through the DOKU Dashboard before the payment methods become available.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   No additional integration is required for merchants already using the supported products.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

For assistance, contact our Care Team at care@doku.com or (021) 1500963.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Thank you and best regards, **DOKU Product Team**

GitBook Assistant

# Customer Static VA - Enhancements | Product Updates | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements.md)
.

Customer Static Virtual Account now comes with several **enhancements** to make it easier for merchants to view and track customer transactions.

GitBook Assistant

**With this enhancement, merchants can view the transaction list directly on the Customer Detail page, as well as access the Transaction Report to view transaction details associated with the customer.**

GitBook Assistant

Additionally, Static Virtual Account is now **compatible with BNI**, giving merchants more flexibility in managing customer payments.

GitBook Assistant

These enhancements make it easier for merchants to connect customers with their transactions and track transaction details through the Transaction Report.

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252FJMNV6pzsdM8dW63j4kHu%252FMonthly%2520Product%2520August_%2523%2520KV6%2520%281%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D653d5863-18c7-4ced-9302-b72798922a1d&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=ccae6d589e252f62c663d71316486f65&sv=3)

* * *

### 🔍 What’s New?[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#whats-new)

#### **Customer-Level Transaction List**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#customer-level-transaction-list)

Each customer profile now includes a dedicated **Transaction List** showing the transactions associated with that customer.

GitBook Assistant

Merchants can view:

GitBook Assistant

*   Inovice ID
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Amount
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Transaction Date
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

This provides a more complete view of the customer's payment activity without requiring merchants to search for individual transactions separately.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Transaction Report**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#transaction-report)

Merchants can also access the **Transaction Report** to view transaction details associated with a specific customer.

GitBook Assistant

This makes it easier to track customer transactions and review their payment activity in more detail.

GitBook Assistant

#### **BNI Compatibility**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#bni-compatibility)

**Customer Static VA is now compatible with BNI**, expanding the available bank options for merchants using Customer Static VA.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 💡 Why It Matters[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#why-it-matters)

Previously, merchants could view customer information but had limited visibility into the customer's transaction history directly from the customer profile.

GitBook Assistant

CSVA V2 connects these two pieces of information, helping merchants understand **who the customer is and what transactions they have made** in one place.

GitBook Assistant

*   **Better customer visibility:** understand transaction activity at an individual customer level.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Faster transaction review:** check customer payment history directly from the profile.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Easier monitoring:** quickly identify successful and failed transactions.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Less manual searching:** no need to look for transactions separately.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### 🚀 How It Works[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#how-it-works)

#### 🔄 Before[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#before)

Merchants could access customer information, but transaction activity was not directly available within the customer profile.

GitBook Assistant

To review a customer's transactions, merchants had to search for the relevant transactions separately.

GitBook Assistant

#### 🆕 After[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#after)

Merchants can now:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Open the **DOKU Dashboard**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Go to **Customers**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Select the customer they want to review.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Open the **Transaction List**.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Review the customer's transaction amount and status.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

The customer's transaction activity is now available directly from their profile.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 💼 **Key Features**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#key-features)

*   Transaction List within each customer profile
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Customer-level transaction visibility
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Transaction amount information
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Transaction status information
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Visibility into successful and failed transactions
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Direct access through **Dashboard → Customers**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   No separate transaction search required for basic customer-level review
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### **📌 Key Details**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements#key-details)

*   Transaction activity is displayed based on the selected customer.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Merchants can access the Transaction List from the customer's profile.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   The feature is designed to provide **customer-level transaction visibility**, rather than replacing the main transaction monitoring experience.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

For assistance, contact our Care Team at care@doku.com or (021) 1500963.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Thank you and best regards, **DOKU Product Team**

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/august-2026/payment-link-dynamic-currency-conversion.md). # Payment Link: Dynamic Currency Conversion Effective Date: 7th August, 2026 DCC (Dynamic Currency Conversion) is now available on \*\*DOKU Payment Link\*\*. Merchants can enable DCC directly when creating a Payment Link by turning on the \*\*“Use DCC”\*\* toggle. Once enabled, customers can view the estimated payment amount in a familiar currency, while the actual payment is still processed using the original currency of the Payment Link. \*\*Supported currencies:\*\* USD, SGD, MYR, JPY, AUD, RUB, and RMB.

![](https://2961485474-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%2Fuploads%2FSD9VX1CNiVZZvZtEcTkY%2FMonthly%20Product%20August_%23%20KV7%20(1).png?alt=media&token=ef1b1bc3-b460-4b46-9013-2f0caead7255)

\*\*\* ### 🔍 What’s New? DCC adds a currency conversion view to Payment Link, allowing customers to check the estimated value of their payment in another currency. For example, a Payment Link priced at \*\*IDR 1,000,000\*\* can be displayed as an \*\*estimated value\*\* in \*\*USD, SGD, MYR, JPY, AUD, RUB, or RMB\*\* based on the customer's preference. The selected currency is only used to \*\*display the estimated amount\*\*. It does not change the original transaction currency. \*\*\* ### 💡 Why It Matters DCC helps international customers better understand the value of a Payment Link before making a payment. It provides a more familiar price reference without changing the actual payment currency. #### \*\*For Merchants\*\* \* Make Payment Links easier to understand for international customers \* Improve the payment experience for customers from different countries \* Support businesses that serve international customers without changing their existing payment currency #### \*\*For Customers\*\* \* See an estimated price in a familiar currency \* Understand the payment amount more easily \* Make payment decisions with better price visibility \*\*\* ### 🚀 How It Works #### 🔄 Before Without DCC, customers see the Payment Link amount only in the original transaction currency. For example: > \*\*IDR 2,000,000\*\* International customers need to estimate the value themselves in their preferred currency. #### 🆕 After With DCC enabled, customers can view an estimated value in another supported currency. For example: > \*\*IDR 2,000,000\*\*\\ > ≈ \*\*SGD 143.59\*\* Customers can switch between supported currencies to better understand the payment amount. \*\*The actual payment remains in IDR.\*\* \*\*\* ### ⚙️ How to Enable Enabling DCC is simple: 1. Create a \*\*Payment Link\*\*. 2. Turn on the \*\*“Use DCC”\*\* toggle. 3. Create and share the Payment Link with your customer. 4. Customers can select a supported currency to view the estimated payment amount. 5. Payment is completed using the \*\*original Payment Link currency\*\*. \*\*\* ### 🎯 \*\*Potential Use Cases\*\* DCC is particularly useful for merchants that serve international customers, such as: \* \*\*Hotels & Hospitality\*\* — international guests can easily understand room or booking prices. \* \*\*Tourism & Travel\*\* — help overseas customers understand tour, ticket, or travel-related payments. \* \*\*Retail & E-commerce\*\* — make product prices easier to understand for international buyers. \* \*\*Education & Events\*\* — support international participants paying through Payment Link. \* \*\*Professional Services\*\* — provide clearer pricing for overseas customers. \*\*\* ### ⚠️ Important Notes DCC \*\*does not mean the merchant receives payment in USD, SGD, JPY, or other supported currencies\*\*. The selected currency is only used to show the customer an estimated equivalent value. > \*\*DCC = Currency visibility, not currency conversion of the actual payment.\*\* \*\*\* For assistance, contact our Care Team at or (021) 1500963. \*\*\* Thank you and best regards,\\ \*\*DOKU Product Team\*\* --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. GitBook is the documentation platform designed so that both humans and AI agents can read, navigate, and reason over technical content effectively. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/customer-static-va-enhancements.md). # Customer Static VA - Enhancements Effective Date: 16th July, 2026 Customer Static Virtual Account now comes with several \*\*enhancements\*\* to make it easier for merchants to view and track customer transactions. \*\*With this enhancement, merchants can view the transaction list directly on the Customer Detail page, as well as access the Transaction Report to view transaction details associated with the customer.\*\* Additionally, Static Virtual Account is now \*\*compatible with BNI\*\*, giving merchants more flexibility in managing customer payments. These enhancements make it easier for merchants to connect customers with their transactions and track transaction details through the Transaction Report.

![](https://2961485474-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%2Fuploads%2FJMNV6pzsdM8dW63j4kHu%2FMonthly%20Product%20August_%23%20KV6%20(1).png?alt=media&token=653d5863-18c7-4ced-9302-b72798922a1d)

\*\*\* ### 🔍 What’s New? #### \*\*Customer-Level Transaction List\*\* Each customer profile now includes a dedicated \*\*Transaction List\*\* showing the transactions associated with that customer. Merchants can view: \* Inovice ID \* Amount \* Transaction Date This provides a more complete view of the customer's payment activity without requiring merchants to search for individual transactions separately. #### \*\*Transaction Report\*\* Merchants can also access the \*\*Transaction Report\*\* to view transaction details associated with a specific customer. This makes it easier to track customer transactions and review their payment activity in more detail. #### \*\*BNI Compatibility\*\* \*\*Customer Static VA is now compatible with BNI\*\*, expanding the available bank options for merchants using Customer Static VA. \*\*\* ### 💡 Why It Matters Previously, merchants could view customer information but had limited visibility into the customer's transaction history directly from the customer profile. CSVA V2 connects these two pieces of information, helping merchants understand \*\*who the customer is and what transactions they have made\*\* in one place. \* \*\*Better customer visibility:\*\* understand transaction activity at an individual customer level. \* \*\*Faster transaction review:\*\* check customer payment history directly from the profile. \* \*\*Easier monitoring:\*\* quickly identify successful and failed transactions. \* \*\*Less manual searching:\*\* no need to look for transactions separately. \*\*\* ### 🚀 How It Works #### 🔄 Before Merchants could access customer information, but transaction activity was not directly available within the customer profile. To review a customer's transactions, merchants had to search for the relevant transactions separately. #### 🆕 After Merchants can now: 1. Open the \*\*DOKU Dashboard\*\*. 2. Go to \*\*Customers\*\*. 3. Select the customer they want to review. 4. Open the \*\*Transaction List\*\*. 5. Review the customer's transaction amount and status. The customer's transaction activity is now available directly from their profile. \*\*\* ### 💼 \*\*Key Features\*\* \* Transaction List within each customer profile \* Customer-level transaction visibility \* Transaction amount information \* Transaction status information \* Visibility into successful and failed transactions \* Direct access through \*\*Dashboard → Customers\*\* \* No separate transaction search required for basic customer-level review \*\*\* ### \*\*📌 Key Details\*\* \* Transaction activity is displayed based on the selected customer. \* Merchants can access the Transaction List from the customer's profile. \* The feature is designed to provide \*\*customer-level transaction visibility\*\*, rather than replacing the main transaction monitoring experience. \*\*\* For assistance, contact our Care Team at or (021) 1500963. \*\*\* Thank you and best regards,\\ \*\*DOKU Product Team\*\* --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. \> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-payment-method-va-bank-bjb-and-bank-sinarmas.md). # New Payment Method - VA Bank BJB & Bank Sinarmas Effective Date: July, 2026 DOKU is expanding its Virtual Account (VA) payment methods with the addition of \*\*Bank BJB\*\* and \*\*Bank Sinarmas\*\*, giving merchants more payment options while helping customers pay through their preferred banks. With this update, merchants can easily activate the new payment methods through the DOKU Dashboard and immediately offer broader payment coverage to their customers. \[\*\*Learn How to Activate It\*\*\](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/manage-payment-methods)

![](https://2961485474-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%2Fuploads%2FWR7YABDa6xmjcibpy8Lt%2FMonthly%20Product%20Update%20July_7.png?alt=media&token=dd292d71-37f0-40b7-bd88-5cfd7db09e9b)

\*\*\* ### 🔍 What’s New? Two new Virtual Account payment methods are now available on DOKU: \*\*Bank Sinarmas Virtual Account\*\* Available for merchants using DOKU Checkout, including Payment Link and Digital Catalog. Once activated, customers will automatically see Bank Sinarmas as one of the available payment options during checkout. \*\*Bank BJB Virtual Account\*\* Available for merchants using Direct API integration, allowing businesses with their own payment interface to offer Bank BJB Virtual Accounts to customers. \*\*\* ### 💡 Why It Matters Every business serves different customer segments, and payment preferences often vary by region and banking ecosystem. By adding Bank BJB and Bank Sinarmas Virtual Accounts, merchants can provide customers with more convenient payment options while reducing the likelihood of abandoned payments caused by limited banking choices or interbank transfer fees. This expansion also helps businesses increase payment accessibility, particularly for customers in \*\*West Java and Banten\*\* through Bank BJB and customers within the broader \*\*Sinarmas ecosystem\*\*. \*\*\* ### \*\*🚀 Updated Experiences\*\* \*\*🔄 Before\*\* Previously, merchants could only offer the existing Virtual Account payment methods supported by DOKU. Customers banking with Bank BJB or Bank Sinarmas often needed to transfer through other banks, potentially incurring additional transfer fees or choosing alternative payment methods. \*\*🆕 After\*\* Merchants can now activate Bank BJB and Bank Sinarmas Virtual Accounts directly from the DOKU Dashboard. Once activated: \* Bank Sinarmas automatically appears on supported DOKU Checkout experiences. \* Direct API merchants can begin accepting payments through Bank BJB. \* Customers enjoy more payment choices using their preferred banking services. \*\*\* ### ⚙️ How It Works Activating the new Virtual Account channels only takes a few steps: 1. Log in to the \*\*DOKU Dashboard\*\*. 2. Navigate to \*\*Settings\*\*. 3. Select \*\*Services\*\*. 4. Click \*\*Add Services\*\*. 5. Under \*\*Bank Transfer\*\*, select: \* \*\*Virtual Account Bank BJB\*\* \* \*\*Virtual Account Bank Sinarmas\*\* 6. Save your changes to activate the selected payment methods. Once activated, the new payment methods will become available based on your integration type. \*\*\* ### \*\*💼 Key Feature\*\* With this update, merchants can: \* Accept payments through \*\*Bank BJB Virtual Account\*\*. \* Accept payments through \*\*Bank Sinarmas Virtual Account\*\*. \* Enable new Virtual Account channels directly from the DOKU Dashboard. \* Expand payment coverage across more Indonesian banking customers. \* Increase payment convenience without changing existing payment workflows. \*\*\* ### 📌 Things to Know With this update, merchants can: \* \*\*Bank Sinarmas Virtual Account\*\* is available for merchants using \*\*DOKU Checkout\*\*, including Payment Link and Digital Catalog. \* \*\*Bank BJB Virtual Account\*\* is available for merchants using \*\*Direct API\*\* integration. \* Activation is required through the DOKU Dashboard before the payment methods become available. \* No additional integration is required for merchants already using the supported products. \*\*\* For assistance, contact our Care Team at or (021) 1500963. \*\*\* Thank you and best regards,\\ \*\*DOKU Product Team\*\* --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/asia-chatbot.md). # ASIA Chatbot Effective Date: 29th July, 2026 \*\*ASIA\*\* is now more than a chatbot that answers merchant questions. With the latest update, ASIA can \*\*help merchants check information and complete selected tasks directly from chat\*\*, making it easier to get things done and saving time on navigating the DOKU Dashboard. \*\*ASIA is your Dashboard assistant\*\* helping answer your needs without having to read through documentation or submit a ticket to DOKU Care for every request. The update includes \*\*transaction insights, settlement checks, payment method activation, automatic Care escalation, and faster responses\*\*, giving merchants a simpler and more efficient way to manage common payment-related tasks.

![](https://2961485474-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%2Fuploads%2FLAu2UPpLxzMlgQnpezW5%2FMonthly%20Product%20August_%23%20KV5%20(1).png?alt=media&token=c5ba19fa-e6ec-48e2-ae7e-89e36fad7de5)

\*\*\* ### 🔍 What’s New? #### \*\*1. Transaction Assistance\*\* Merchants can ask ASIA to help with transaction-related information, including: \* Transaction details and payment status \* Payment journey \* GMV calculation \* Payment method usage For example: > “What payment method was used the most in the last 7 days?” \[\*\*Also available on DOKU Agentic Payment\*\*\](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/doku-mcp-server) #### \*\*2. Settlement Assistance\*\* ASIA can help merchants check: \* Settlement status \* Whether a settlement is pending or completed \* Settlement date Merchants can also ask ASIA to check the settlement status of specific transactions. \[\*\*Also available on DOKU Agentic Payment\*\*\](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/doku-mcp-server) #### \*\*3. Payment Method Activation\*\* Merchants can activate available payment methods directly through chat. For example: > “I want to activate QRIS.” Instead of navigating to \*\*Settings → Service\*\*, merchants can simply ask ASIA to help with the activation. #### \*\*4. Automatic Care Escalation\*\* If ASIA cannot resolve a merchant's issue, it can automatically create a \*\*Freshdesk ticket\*\* and escalate the case to the \*\*Care team\*\*. This means merchants no longer need to manually submit a form or send an email to create a support ticket. #### \*\*5. Smarter & Faster Responses\*\* ASIA's knowledge base has been updated with the latest product and operational information, improving response accuracy. Average response time has also improved from approximately \*\*9 seconds to 5–6 seconds\*\*. \*\*\* ### 💡 Why It Matters Previously, merchants needed to navigate different Dashboard menus or contact the Care team for certain requests. With the latest ASIA update, merchants can get information and complete selected tasks \*\*through one conversational interface\*\*, making common interactions faster and easier. with key benefits: \* \*\*Less navigation:\*\* get help directly from chat \* \*\*Faster information access:\*\* quickly check transactions and settlements \* \*\*Simpler activation:\*\* activate available payment methods through chat \* \*\*Seamless support:\*\* unresolved issues can be automatically escalated to Care \* \*\*Faster responses:\*\* improved knowledge base and response time \*\*\* ### 🚀 How It Works \*\*ASIA uses MCP Server to connect its conversational experience with selected DOKU capabilities, including Transaction and Settlement.\*\* This enables merchants to retrieve relevant transaction and settlement information directly through their conversations with ASIA. This allows ASIA to go beyond answering questions and \*\*retrieve information or perform selected actions based on the merchant's request.\*\* #### 🔄 Before Merchants typically need to: 1. Open ASIA the DOKU Dashboard. 2. Using chatbot for only general questions. 3. If ask for action realted, navigate to the relevant menu. 4. Search for transaction or settlement information. 5. Go to \*\*Settings → Service\*\* to manage payment methods. 6. Contact Care manually if they need further assistance. #### 🆕 After Merchants can simply: 1. Open \*\*ASIA\*\* from the DOKU Dashboard. 2. Ask a question or request an action. 3. ASIA retrieves the relevant information or performs the available action. 4. If ASIA cannot resolve the issue, a \*\*Freshdesk ticket\*\* is automatically created and escalated to Care. \*\*\* ### 💼 \*\*Key Features\*\* \* Transaction details and payment status \* Payment journey insights \* GMV calculation \* Payment method usage insights \* Settlement status and settlement date \* Payment method activation through chat \* Automatic Freshdesk ticket creation \* Automatic escalation to the Care team \* Updated knowledge base \* Faster average response time of \*\*5–6 seconds\*\* \*\*\* ### \*\*📌 Key Details\*\* \* Payment method activation is available for payment methods that can be activated through ASIA. \* If ASIA cannot resolve an issue, the conversation can be escalated to the Care team through Freshdesk. \* ASIA is available on the \*\*DOKU Dashboard\*\* across supported environments. \*\*\* ### ⚠️ Important Notes ASIA's capabilities will continue to expand. Additional tools and actions may be introduced in future updates, including potential support for other operational requests. For now, merchants should use ASIA for supported transaction, settlement, payment method, and support-related requests. \*\*\* For assistance, contact our Care Team at or (021) 1500963. \*\*\* Thank you and best regards,\\ \*\*DOKU Product Team\*\* --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. GitBook is the documentation platform designed so that both humans and AI agents can read, navigate, and reason over technical content effectively. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/refund-services-whatsapp-channel.md). # Refund Services - Whatsapp Channel Effective Date: July, 2026 Refund notification and passcode now supports delivery through WhatsApp via the official DOKU Business Account, providing a more secure and reliable way for customers to receive refund notifications. This update replaces the previous SMS-based notification flow while maintaining the existing refund process for merchants. \[\*\*Learn about Refund Services\*\*\](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/refund-service)

![](https://2961485474-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%2Fuploads%2FnUrHngl6bgnVomW10ELU%2FMonthly%20Product%20Update%20July_5%20(1).png?alt=media&token=eb9b46ff-7aa6-4786-b59a-19d1f20609ae)

\*\*\* ### 🔍 What’s New? Refund notifications are now delivered through \*\*WhatsApp\*\* instead of SMS for merchants who enable the feature. Customers will receive their refund information directly through the official DOKU WhatsApp account, including the refund link, verification passcode, expiration information, and action button where applicable. Depending on the refund type, customers will receive one of two notification formats: \* \*\*Refund Link\*\* – includes a refund link, verification passcode, expiry information, and \*\*"Receive Refund"\*\* call-to-action. \* \*\*Direct Refund\*\* – includes refund confirmation and a \*\*"Check Status"\*\* button without requiring a passcode. \*\*\* ### 💡 Why It Matters SMS has long been used as the primary notification channel for Refund Link. However, SMS delivery introduces security risks, including the possibility of unauthorized access to refund links or delayed message delivery. By moving notifications to WhatsApp, DOKU provides a more trusted communication channel while strengthening refund security through passcode verification and official business messaging. This enhancement helps merchants improve customer confidence throughout the refund process while reducing potential fraud risks. \*\*\* ### \*\*🚀 Updated Experiences\*\* #### 🔄 Before Previously, customers received Refund Link notifications via SMS. Although functional, SMS delivery presented several challenges: \* Refund notifications could be delayed or fail to reach customers. \* SMS messages were more vulnerable to interception by unauthorized parties. \* Customers relied on SMS to access refund links, creating potential security risks during the refund process. #### 🆕 After Beginning August 10th, 2026, all refund communications will default to WhatsApp and/or email if applicable Refund notifications delivered through the \*\*official DOKU WhatsApp Business Account\*\*. Depending on the refund type, customers will receive either: \* \*\*Refund Link\*\* — including a secure refund link, verification passcode, expiry information, and a \*\*"Receive Refund"\*\* button. \* \*\*Direct Refund\*\* — including a refund confirmation message and a \*\*"Check Status"\*\* button without requiring a passcode. This provides a more secure, reliable, and familiar refund experience while helping reduce the risk of unauthorized refund access. \*\*\* ### 📌 Refund Methods \*\*Non-Direct Refund\*\* They will open the link and enter their own bank account details before the refund is processed. \*\*Best for:\*\* Merchants who do not have the customer's bank account information. \*\*Direct Refund\*\* The customer receives a WhatsApp notification confirming the refund, without needing to enter any additional information. \*\*Best for:\*\* Merchants who already have the customer's bank account information. \*\*\* ### ⚙️ How It Works \* \*\*Enable\*\* WhatsApp Notification for your merchant account. \* Continue sending refund requests using the existing \*\*customer\\\_phone\*\* parameter — no API changes are required. \* DOKU automatically delivers the refund notification through the official WhatsApp Business Account. \* Customers receive the appropriate notification based on the refund type: \* \*\*Refund Link\*\* with passcode verification, or \* \*\*Direct Refund\*\* with refund status confirmation. \* Customers complete the refund process securely through the provided WhatsApp message. \*\*\* ### \*\*💼 Key Feature\*\* \* Deliver Refund Link notifications through the official DOKU WhatsApp Business Account. \* Support dedicated notification formats for Refund Link and Direct Refund. \* Include passcode verification for Refund Link transactions. \* Improve delivery reliability compared to SMS notifications. \* Strengthen refund security by reducing the risk of unauthorized refund access. \* No API changes required for existing Refund Link integrations. \*\*\* ### \*\*📌 Key Details\*\* \* No API parameter changes are required. Merchants continue using the existing \*\*customer\\\_phone\*\* field. \* WhatsApp notification must be \*\*enabled by the merchant\*\* and is not activated by default. \* Once WhatsApp notification is activated, SMS notification will be automatically disabled for that merchant. \* Passcode verification is only applicable to \*\*Refund Link\*\* transactions. Direct Refund notifications do not require a passcode. \*\*\* For assistance, contact our Care Team at or (021) 1500963. \*\*\* Thank you and best regards,\\ \*\*DOKU Product Team\*\* --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. GitBook is the documentation platform designed so that both humans and AI agents can read, navigate, and reason over technical content effectively. # Payment Link

Create a link instantly to collect payments from your customers

<figure><img src="https://1457116080-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%2Fuploads%2FZylgH9wcWj0pi2sjLJPP%2Fpl%20fade.gif?alt=media&amp;token=3f603985-c6c2-4cc4-b1db-334dd373a522" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

## Overview

\*\*Payment Link\*\* enables merchants to accept payments by simply creating and sharing a link to customers via email, messaging apps, social media, or any online platform — no website, app, or coding required. Payment Link can be generated through 3 platforms:

1. Website via \[DOKU Dashboard\](https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/payment-link?utm\_source=docs)
2. Mobile app via \[Juragan DOKU\](/mobile-apps/juragan-doku.md#installation)\\
   :information\\\_source:<sub>Only available for Indonesian Business Accounts</sub>
3. Social messaging app via WhatsApp\\
   :information\\\_source:<sub>Only available for Indonesian Business Accounts</sub>

<table data-view="cards"><thead><tr><th></th><th></th><th data-hidden data-card-target data-type="content-ref"></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Create Payment Link</strong></td><td>Quickly accept payments in 3 easy steps</td><td><a href="/accept-payments/no-integration-products/payment-link/create-payment-link.md">Create Payment Link</a></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Manage Payment Link</strong></td><td>Track payment status, send notification, and manage payment link report  </td><td><a href="/accept-payments/no-integration-products/payment-link/manage-payment-link.md">Manage Payment Link</a></td></tr></tbody></table>

### Link Types

{% tabs %}
{% tab title="Single Payment Link" %}
\*\*Single Payment Link\*\* is used to collect one-time or recurring (partial) payments from a specific customer. Once full payment has been made, the link can no longer be used to accept payments and becomes inaccessible. It is ideal for sending invoices for products or services sold to individual customers.

Single Payment Link cannot be reused after payment completion, but you can duplicate and edit the link for other customers.
{% endtab %}

{% tab title="Multiple Payment Link" %}
\*\*Multiple Payment Link\*\* is used to collect several payments using the same link. It is ideal for businesses that sell identical products or services to multiple customers.

The link remains active and reusable until it reaches its specified expiration date or transaction usage limit. Merchants can configure a transaction limit per link or allow unlimited transactions based on their business needs.
{% endtab %}
{% endtabs %}

|                                   | Single Payment Link                                                                   | Multiple Payment Link                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Description                       | Used for collecting one-time or recurring (partial) payments from a specific customer | Used for selling a product, a service, or accepting donations from multiple customers |
| Customer                          | Specific individuals or businesses                                                    | Multiple individuals or businesses (anyone with the link)                             |
| Reusability                       | Cannot be reused after full payment; can be duplicated and edited for a new customer  | Reusable until expiration or usage limit is reached                                   |
| Use Cases                         | Sending invoices of a product/service to a single customer                            | Selling products/services to multiple customers; fundraising or mass sales            |
| Partial Payments or Payment Plans | ✅ Supported                                                                           | ❌ Not Supported                                                                       |
| Promo/Discounts                   | ✅ Supported                                                                           | ✅ Supported                                                                           |
| Create from Website               | ✅ Supported                                                                           | ✅ Supported                                                                           |
| Create from Mobile App            | ✅ Supported                                                                           | ✅ Supported                                                                           |
| Create from WhatsApp              | ✅ Supported                                                                           | ✅ Supported                                                                           |

### Order Types

{% tabs %}
{% tab title="Amount & Description" %}
\*\*Amount & Description\*\*: Merchants create a Payment Link by entering the payment amount and a description of the product or service. Customers simply review the preset information and complete the payment without modifying any details.
{% endtab %}

{% tab title="Items & Amount" %}
\*\*Items & Amount:\*\* Merchants define multiple products or services with assigned quantities and prices. Customers can select one or more items and adjust the quantity during checkout, based on the available limits set by the merchant.
{% endtab %}

{% tab title="Accept Any Amount" %}
\*\*Accept Any Amount:\*\* Merchants create a Payment Link where customers are free to decide the amount they want to pay. Only a description is preset; no amount is fixed in advance.
{% endtab %}
{% endtabs %}

|                                     | Amount & Description                                                                                                                                     | Items & Amount                                                                                                                              | Accept Any Amount                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Description                         | Simple payment by entering only the amount and description                                                                                               | Input multiple products/services with quantity and price breakdown shown at checkout                                                        | Yes, by deciding the amount to be paid.                                                                                |
| Customers Choose What to Pay (PWYW) | Yes, with Partial Payments                                                                                                                               | Yes, by selecting quantity of products/services                                                                                             | Yes, by deciding the amount to be paid                                                                                 |
| Set Minimum and Maximum Amount      | Yes, with Partial Payments                                                                                                                               | Yes, by setting minimum/maximum quantity for each product/service                                                                           | No, freely entered by customers                                                                                        |
| Use Cases                           | Professional services (e.g., consulting fees, legal services, design work), subscription invoicing with fixed pricing, single project or service billing | Online stores offering multiple products, service packages with optional add-ons, order-based businesses such as catering or event services | Donation campaigns, crowdfunding, charity fundraising, tipping systems, membership contributions with flexible amounts |
| Single Payment Link                 | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                              | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                 | ✅ Supported                                                                                                            |
| Multiple Payment Link               | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                              | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                 | ✅ Supported                                                                                                            |
| Create from Website                 | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                              | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                 | ✅ Supported                                                                                                            |
| Create from Mobile App              | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                              | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                 | ❌ Not Supported                                                                                                        |
| Create from WhatsApp                | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                              | ✅ Supported                                                                                                                                 | ❌ Not Supported                                                                                                        |

### Customer Info Types

{% tabs %}
{% tab title="Collect Customer" %}
\*\*Collect Customer:\*\* Merchants can specify required customer details such as name, email, telephone number, and address. Customers input this information before completing their payment. This method is ideal for merchants who need customer data for transaction tracking or record keeping.
{% endtab %}

{% tab title="Select Customer" %}
\*\*Select Customer:\*\* Merchants can select from previously registered customers or add new customer information manually when creating a Payment Link. This option is suitable for invoicing or billing scenarios where the merchant manages a customer database and needs to associate transactions with specific customers.
{% endtab %}
{% endtabs %}

|                                   | Collect Customer                                                                                          | Select Customer                                                                                      |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Description                       | Customers input their personal information (name, email, phone number, address) before completing payment | Merchants select existing registered customers or add new customer data during Payment Link creation |
| Use Cases                         | Fast creation of Payment Links while still collecting basic customer information for tracking and reports | Ideal for invoicing or billing existing customers, enabling better transaction history management    |
| Partial Payments or Payment Plans | ❌ Not Supported                                                                                           | ✅ Supported                                                                                          |
| Single Payment Link               | ✅ Supported                                                                                               | ✅ Supported                                                                                          |
| Multiple Payment Link             | ✅ Supported                                                                                               | ❌ Not Supported                                                                                      |
| Create from Website               | ✅ Supported                                                                                               | ✅ Supported                                                                                          |
| Create from Mobile App            | ✅ Supported                                                                                               | ✅ Supported                                                                                          |
| Create from WhatsApp              | ✅ Supported                                                                                               | ✅ Supported                                                                                          |

### Creation Methods

{% tabs %}
{% tab title="Single Creation" %}
\*\*Single Creation\*\*: Create one Payment Link at a time through the DOKU Dashboard or Juragan DOKU mobile app. This method is suitable for fast payment requests where only one transaction needs to be processed.
{% endtab %}

{% tab title="Bulk Creation" %}
\*\*Bulk Creation\*\*: Generate multiple Payment Links at once by uploading an XLSX file through the DOKU Dashboard. This streamlines mass link creation for merchants handling large customer bases.
{% endtab %}
{% endtabs %}

|                                   | Single Creation                                                     | Bulk Creation                                                              |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Description                       | Create a Payment Link one at a time via the Dashboard or mobile app | Generate multiple Payment Links at once by uploading an XLSX file template |
| Use Cases                         | Suitable for fast, one-off payment requests                         | Suitable for mass invoicing                                                |
| Amount & Description              | ✅ Supported                                                         | ✅ Supported                                                                |
| Items & Amount                    | ✅ Supported                                                         | ✅ Supported                                                                |
| Accept Any Amount                 | ✅ Supported                                                         | ❌ Not Supported                                                            |
| Partial Payments or Payment Plans | ✅ Supported                                                         | ✅ Supported                                                                |
| Single Payment Link               | ✅ Supported                                                         | ✅ Supported                                                                |
| Multiple Payment Link             | ✅ Supported                                                         | ❌ Not Supported                                                            |
| Create from Website               | ✅ Supported                                                         | ✅ Supported                                                                |
| Create from Mobile App            | ✅ Supported                                                         | ❌ Not Supported                                                            |
| Create from WhatsApp              | ✅ Supported                                                         | ❌ Not Supported                                                            |
