# Digital Catalog - Custom Field per Product | Product Updates | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

![Page cover](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252FFXKWSKbnDrgnhO20ldhs%252Fcover%2520%282%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc0770f0d-799d-4129-9e31-c2114ab851b6&width=1248&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=be9454e6950b492edac63845b5b2653a&sv=3)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/june-2026/digital-catalog-custom-field-per-product.md)
.

Digital Catalog now introduces Custom Fields, giving merchants a simple way to collect product-specific order details directly during checkout. This ensures every order arrives complete with the information needed to fulfill it correctly, without manual follow-up.

GitBook Assistant

Beginning 26th June 2026, merchants can configure up to three custom fields per product, and customers will see these fields directly on the product page before completing their purchase.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 🔍 What’s New?[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/june-2026/digital-catalog-custom-field-per-product#whats-new)

Digital Catalog product pages now support configurable **Custom Fields**, completed by customers before checkout.

GitBook Assistant

This update ensures:

GitBook Assistant

*   Accurate, product-specific order details collected upfront
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Reduced manual follow-up via chat or email
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Greater flexibility in managing how and when products go live
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

### The New Checkout Flow[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/june-2026/digital-catalog-custom-field-per-product#the-new-checkout-flow)

#### 🔄 **Before This Update**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/june-2026/digital-catalog-custom-field-per-product#before-this-update)

Today, the purchase flow goes straight from product page to checkout, with no way to collect additional order-specific details. While functional, this creates a gap for merchants selling personalized, customizable, or booking-based products and important details like engraving text, variant preference, or delivery instructions can only be collected manually after payment.

GitBook Assistant

Steps:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Customer visits product page
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Customer proceeds directly to checkout
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Customer completes payment
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Merchant manually follows up to collect missing order details (if needed)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252F9BmXWiqjFgaPqM6PN4nb%252Fdc1%2520%281%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D8133daef-cc38-4724-a315-d542bf743b61&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=1d551b7aaabdbe425f29f406aef98b9c&sv=3)

#### 🆕 **After This Update**[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/june-2026/digital-catalog-custom-field-per-product#after-this-update)

With Custom Fields, the purchase flow now includes a step for customers to provide product-specific details before checkout and ensuring merchants receive complete order information from the start.

GitBook Assistant

Steps:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Customer visits product page
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Custom fields are displayed (e.g., engraving text, product preference, participant name)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Customer completes required information
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Customer proceeds to checkout and completes payment
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Merchant receives order details together with the transaction
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

**Benefit**

GitBook Assistant

Merchants get exactly the information they need to fulfill the order right — no extra steps, no manual chasing.

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F2961485474-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%252Fuploads%252F5624Onp5r40bSQ9t9rUM%252Fdc2%2520%283%29.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D10fc98b1-1218-4fe7-b4db-742dff46eac7&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=0b5bd2351e570c88f5af4537d08ce547&sv=3)

* * *

### ✅ Key Details[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/june-2026/digital-catalog-custom-field-per-product#key-details)

**Up to 3 Custom Fields per Product** Merchants can configure up to three custom fields for each product in their Digital Catalog.

GitBook Assistant

**Two Field Types Available** Merchants can choose between Text Input (for open-ended responses like engraving text or delivery notes) or Dropdown Select (for predefined options like size or variant).

GitBook Assistant

**Optional or Required** Each custom field can be marked as required, ensuring customers cannot proceed to checkout without providing essential information.

GitBook Assistant

**Product Management Controls** Products can be saved as Draft, Published when ready, or Unpublished when no longer available.

GitBook Assistant

**Verification Workflow** Newly published or edited products go through a verification process before becoming visible in the Digital Catalog.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

### 📌 Important Notes[](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/june-2026/digital-catalog-custom-field-per-product#important-notes)

*   Products without custom fields will continue to follow the existing purchase flow with no additional steps.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   We recommend reviewing your product catalog and identifying which products would benefit from custom fields — especially personalized items, bookings, or made-to-order products.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   For assistance setting up Custom Fields, contact our Care Team at [care@doku.com](mailto:care@doku.com)
     or (021) 1500963.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

Thank you and best regards, **DOKU Product Team**

GitBook Assistant

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.doku.com/changelog/july-2026/new-tools-ai-agent-toolkit.md).

# New Tools - AI Agent Toolkit

Effective Date: July, 2026

AI Agent Toolkit is a developer tool that integrates DOKU's API specifications directly into AI coding assistants such as Claude Code and Cursor (The installation guide is available upon request. Please contact our Sales Team). Instead of manually reading documentation and writing integration logic from scratch, developers can generate production-ready DOKU API implementation through simple prompts inside their development environment.

\[<mark style="color:$primary;">\*\*Learn How to Integrate in DOKU API References\*\*</mark>\](https://developers.doku.com/developer-kit/ai-agent-toolkit)

<figure><img src="https://2961485474-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2F5XrPPWlRNNTQG72DaVGd%2Fuploads%2F7Ia0pvaGTEnJpxp2JPKa%2FMonthly%20Product%20Update%20July\_6.png?alt=media&amp;token=f3b491ba-1e6a-465e-8169-81432ff36cac" alt=""><figcaption></figcaption></figure>

\*\*\*

### 🔍 What’s New?

DOKU now provides an AI Agent Toolkit that allows developers to build DOKU integrations using AI-assisted coding workflows.

Once installed, the toolkit automatically retrieves the latest DOKU API specifications, understands the developer's project environment, and generates implementation-ready source code.

The toolkit supports code generation for:

\* Payment API integration
\* Signature generation and validation
\* Webhook handlers
\* Client libraries
\* Authentication logic
\* Production readiness checklist

This enables developers to integrate DOKU APIs without manually translating documentation into code.

\*\*\*

### 💡 Why It Matters

Building a payment integration often requires developers to spend significant time understanding API documentation, implementing authentication logic, validating signatures, and troubleshooting integration issues.

As payment APIs continue to evolve, maintaining up-to-date implementations also becomes increasingly challenging.

AI Agent Toolkit simplifies this development process by allowing developers to generate implementation-ready code directly from DOKU's latest API specifications, reducing manual effort while improving development speed and consistency.

\*\*\*

### \*\*🚀 Updated Experiences\*\*

#### 🔄 Before&#x20;

Previously, developers needed to manually:

\* Read and understand DOKU API documentation.
\* Implement authentication and signature generation.
\* Build webhook handlers from scratch.
\* Create API requests manually for testing.
\* Troubleshoot implementation issues caused by outdated documentation or coding mistakes.

#### 🆕 After

With AI Agent Toolkit, developers can simply describe the integration they want inside Claude, Curosor, or Cursor The installation guide is available upon request. Please contact our Sales Team).

The toolkit automatically references the latest DOKU API specifications and generates production-ready source code based on the project's language and framework, allowing developers to focus on building their applications instead of repetitive implementation work.

\*\*\*

### ⚙️ How It Works

1. Install the DOKU AI Agent Toolkit in Claude, Code, or Cursor (Request through Sales Team).
2. Connect the toolkit to your development project.
3. Describe the integration you want using natural language prompts.
4. The toolkit retrieves the latest DOKU API specifications.
5. Source code is automatically generated based on your project's programming language and framework.
6. Review, customize, and deploy the generated implementation into your application.

\[<mark style="color:$primary;">\*\*Learn More in DOKU API References\*\*</mark>\](https://developers.doku.com/developer-kit/ai-agent-toolkit)

\*\*\*

### \*\*💼 Key Feature\*\*

\* Generate production-ready DOKU API integration code.
\* Automatically retrieve the latest DOKU API specifications.
\* Detect project language and framework automatically.
\* Generate signature generation and validation logic.
\* Create webhook handlers and API client implementations.
\* Reduce manual implementation and repetitive development tasks.
\* Improve consistency across DOKU integrations.

\*\*\*

### \*\*📌 Key Details\*\*

\* AI Agent Toolkit is intended for \*\*developers and technical teams\*\* during the integration phase.
\* The toolkit generates \*\*source code\*\*, not actual payment transactions or payment results.
\* Generated code still needs to be deployed and tested using the DOKU Sandbox or production environment.
\* Existing merchant integrations are \*\*not affected\*\* by this release.
\* The toolkit currently supports \*\*Claude,\*\* \*\*Cursor, and Codex (Request trough Sales Team)\*\*.

\*\*\*

For assistance, contact our Care Team at <care@doku.com> or (021) 1500963.

\*\*\*

Thank you and best regards,\\
\*\*DOKU Product Team\*\*


---

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/direct-api.md).

# Direct API

Use your own custom payment page to collect payments

If you wish to implement your own payment page, DOKU provides REST API that you can use to directly integrate with us. We provide code library in various programming languages to help you integrate.&#x20;

With Direct API, you are enabled to create customized payment flows and integrate them directly into your website while still adhering to security and compliance standards. The following are some benefits of integrating with Direct API:

| Benefits                      | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Brand Consistency             | Merchants can maintain a consistent brand experience throughout the entire customer journey, including the checkout process. Customization allows for the i\*\*ncorporation of brand colors, logos, and styles\*\*, reinforcing brand identity.                                                                            |
| Seamless User Experience      | Integration directly into the website ensures a seamless user experience. Customers \*\*stay on the merchant's site\*\* from product selection to payment confirmation, reducing friction and potential drop-offs in the conversion funnel.                                                                                |
| Flexibility and Customization | Developers have the flexibility to design and implement payment flows that align with the specific requirements of the business. Customization \*\*extends to the layout, user interface elements, and user interactions\*\* during the payment process.                                                                   |
| Control Over User Interface   | Merchants and developers have complete control over the look and feel of the payment interface. This control is valuable for creating \*\*user-friendly and intuitive payment forms\*\*, optimizing the checkout process for increased conversions.                                                                        |
| Security Compliance           | DOKU handles the complexities of security compliance, especially compliance with the Payment Card Industry Data Security Standard (PCI DSS). By using DOKUs APIs, merchants can ensure that \*\*sensitive payment information is handled securely\*\* without having to manage intricate security requirements themselves. |
| Scalability                   | DOKU's infrastructure is designed for scalability. Merchants can handle growing transaction volumes \*\*without worrying about the technical challenges\*\* associated with scaling payment processing capabilities.                                                                                                       |

{% hint style="info" %}
You can try Direct API by using our demo \[here\](https://sandbox.doku.com/demo/direct-api), or immediately integrate with our API by following the guide \[here\](https://developers.doku.com/accept-payment/direct-api).
{% endhint %}

2.  Digital Catalog
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
    *   You can easily list all your products and services such as reserving and booking airline tickets, hotel and car services.
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    

For Accepting Payments

GitBook Assistant

# Direct API | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/direct-api.md)
.

If you wish to implement your own payment page, DOKU provides REST API that you can use to directly integrate with us. We provide code library in various programming languages to help you integrate.

GitBook Assistant

With Direct API, you are enabled to create customized payment flows and integrate them directly into your website while still adhering to security and compliance standards. The following are some benefits of integrating with Direct API:

GitBook Assistant

Benefits

Description

Brand Consistency

GitBook Assistant

Merchants can maintain a consistent brand experience throughout the entire customer journey, including the checkout process. Customization allows for the i**ncorporation of brand colors, logos, and styles**, reinforcing brand identity.

GitBook Assistant

Seamless User Experience

GitBook Assistant

Integration directly into the website ensures a seamless user experience. Customers **stay on the merchant's site** from product selection to payment confirmation, reducing friction and potential drop-offs in the conversion funnel.

GitBook Assistant

Flexibility and Customization

GitBook Assistant

Developers have the flexibility to design and implement payment flows that align with the specific requirements of the business. Customization **extends to the layout, user interface elements, and user interactions** during the payment process.

GitBook Assistant

Control Over User Interface

GitBook Assistant

Merchants and developers have complete control over the look and feel of the payment interface. This control is valuable for creating **user-friendly and intuitive payment forms**, optimizing the checkout process for increased conversions.

GitBook Assistant

Security Compliance

GitBook Assistant

DOKU handles the complexities of security compliance, especially compliance with the Payment Card Industry Data Security Standard (PCI DSS). By using DOKUs APIs, merchants can ensure that **sensitive payment information is handled securely** without having to manage intricate security requirements themselves.

GitBook Assistant

Scalability

GitBook Assistant

DOKU's infrastructure is designed for scalability. Merchants can handle growing transaction volumes **without worrying about the technical challenges** associated with scaling payment processing capabilities.

GitBook Assistant

You can try Direct API by using our demo [here](https://sandbox.doku.com/demo/direct-api)
, or immediately integrate with our API by following the guide [here](https://developers.doku.com/accept-payment/direct-api)
.

GitBook Assistant

[PreviousManage Checkout Orders](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/doku-checkout/manage-checkout-orders)
[Nexte-Commerce and Plugins](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/integration-tools/e-commerce-and-plugins)

# Juragan DOKU | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku.md)
.

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252Fb9PYTrsEPzTM96FWwy9z%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9724d876-7b8b-4ee5-9ad8-a709e5269e24&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=bcd96459763d67f36f5a799851975fa3&sv=3)

Overview[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#overview)

--------------------------------------------------------------------

Juragan DOKU is a mobile app that enables small-to-medium businesses to accept payments both online and offline by generating payment links or showcasing your products through a catalog. Juragan DOKU simplifies your business with a comprehensive transaction reporting feature to keep track of all your transactions in one place with a detailed and easy-to-understand report.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Key Features[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#key-features)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Payment Link[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#payment-link)

#### Create Orders with Payment Links in an Instant[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#create-orders-with-payment-links-in-an-instant)

Generate payment links that can be seamlessly scanned and shared with your customers. Let them choose from a variety of payment methods, including e-wallets, credit cards, and bank transfers. Payment Link will make it easier for you to track & control all transactions.

GitBook Assistant

The following video is a guide on how to create a Payment Link with Juragan DOKU.[](https://youtube.com/shorts/yKtcMkAQmjk?si=CjO4jT6l8QZfDnf2https://youtube.com/shorts/QLumJwtuOeA?si=PzZW21kVm2e3L1MYhttps://youtube.com/shorts/P3bOFiZHcJA?si=sQT5853NYXv6Wpor)

GitBook Assistant

[![Logo](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.youtube.com%2Fs%2Fdesktop%2Ff82dea74%2Fimg%2Ffavicon_144x144.png&width=20&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=6d562e612b2db78caa2ab555bbc6a025&sv=3)\- YouTubeyoutube.com](https://youtube.com/watch/yKtcMkAQmjk?si=CjO4jT6l8QZfDnf2)

### QRIS[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#qris)

#### Instant Confirmation through Your Device with Dynamic QRIS[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#instant-confirmation-through-your-device-with-dynamic-qris)

Juragan App allows you to generate dynamic QRIS codes effortlessly. Enjoy instant confirmation right on your phone, ensuring a swift and secure payment process. Embrace transactions with digital payment in the palm of your hand.

GitBook Assistant

The following video is a guide on how to generate a QRIS with Juragan DOKU.[](https://youtube.com/shorts/yKtcMkAQmjk?si=CjO4jT6l8QZfDnf2https://youtube.com/shorts/QLumJwtuOeA?si=PzZW21kVm2e3L1MYhttps://youtube.com/shorts/P3bOFiZHcJA?si=sQT5853NYXv6Wpor)

GitBook Assistant

[![Logo](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.youtube.com%2Fs%2Fdesktop%2F3a93864d%2Fimg%2Ffavicon_144x144.png&width=20&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e8c6a5c1bc1bebb4de936ba1646d9f6e&sv=3)\- YouTubeyoutube.com](https://youtube.com/watch/QLumJwtuOeA?si=PzZW21kVm2e3L1MY)

### Digital Catalog[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#digital-catalog)

#### Effortless Product Management and Order Processing[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#effortless-product-management-and-order-processing)

Transform your selling experience with the Digital Catalog feature on Juragan App. Showcase your products in a visually appealing catalog that you can share with your customers. They can directly make purchases, choosing shipping options such as instant, same- day, or regular shipping. Receive instant notifications through the app for new orders, streamline your order processing, and even request pick-up services to send products to your customers hassle-free.

GitBook Assistant

The following video is a guide on how to use Digital Catalog on Juragan DOKU.

GitBook Assistant

[![Logo](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.youtube.com%2Fs%2Fdesktop%2F3a93864d%2Fimg%2Ffavicon_144x144.png&width=20&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e8c6a5c1bc1bebb4de936ba1646d9f6e&sv=3)\- YouTubeyoutube.com](https://youtube.com/watch/P3bOFiZHcJA?si=sQT5853NYXv6Wpor)

* * *

Installation[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#installation)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Juragan DOKU supports both iOS and Android operating systems. Visit the links below to download and install Juragan DOKU based on the operating system that your phone supports:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Juragan DOKU on [App Store](https://apps.apple.com/id/app/juragan-doku/id1666751965)
     (iOS)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Juragan DOKU on [Play Store](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.doku.juragan&hl=en&gl=US)
     (Android)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

FAQ[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#faq)

----------------------------------------------------------

What is the difference between DOKU Dashboard and Juragan DOKU?[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#what-is-the-difference-between-doku-dashboard-and-juragan-doku)

DOKU Dashboard mainly supports web/browser platforms, while Juragan DOKU only supports mobile platforms (iOS and Android). Despite the difference of platforms, you can log in to DOKU Dashboard and Juragan DOKU with the same DOKU Business Account credentials.

GitBook Assistant

What is the price to install Juragan DOKU app?[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/juragan-doku#what-is-the-price-to-install-juragan-doku-app)

Juragan DOKU App is free on both Play Store and App Store. Furthermore, DOKU does not charge any monthly fee for using Juragan DOKU app. DOKU only charges fee based on every successful transaction.

GitBook Assistant

# Change Password | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-user-account/change-password.md)
.

You can change your DOKU Dashboard password by following the steps below:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Log in to [DOKU Dashboard](https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/login?utm_source=docs)
    , and then access the side navigation bar
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Click the ellipsis icon **( ⋮ )** next to your user name from the menu, and then select **Profile** in the pop-up box
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  **User Profile** page will appear, scroll down to the Change Password section, and then click **Change Password**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Enter your current password and your new password
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

You can't use the same previous passwords twice. The password must be at least 8 characters long with at least 1 uppercase, 1 lowercase, and 1 number.

GitBook Assistant

1.  Click **Change Password** once again to confirm your new password
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  You can now log in with your new password.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

FAQ[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-user-account/change-password#faq)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

I forgot my password. How do I reset my password?[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-user-account/change-password#i-forgot-my-password.-how-do-i-reset-my-password)

If you forgot your DOKU Dashboard password, you may reset your password by following the steps below:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Visit [DOKU Dashboard Login](https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/login)
     page
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Click **Forgot Password?**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Input your email and click **RESEND EMAIL**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Check your inbox, and access the link in the email to reset password
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Input your new password and submit the form
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

You can't use the same previous passwords twice. The password must be at least 8 characters long with at least 1 uppercase, 1 lowercase, and 1 number.

GitBook Assistant

1.  You can now log in with your new password.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

The same guide is applied for [DOKU Sandbox](https://sandbox.doku.com/bo/login)
.

GitBook Assistant

I did not receive any email to reset my password. What should I do?[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-user-account/change-password#i-did-not-receive-any-email-to-reset-my-password.-what-should-i-do)

Please first ensure that the email address is correct. We will not be able to reset your password if the email address is invalid or is unregistered.

GitBook Assistant

If you still have not received an email within 5 minutes, please [submit a support ticket](https://help.doku.com/en/support/tickets/new)
 or send an email to [care@doku.com](mailto:care@doku.com)
.

.

View **Message Reports**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat/bill-broadcast/reports-and-transaction#view-message-reports)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Get a complete view of your message performance through visual chat graphics and detailed message reports. Track the delivery, read status, and engagement metrics for each broadcasted message, helping you monitor effectiveness and optimize communication.

GitBook Assistant

spinner

* * *

**View Transaction List**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat/bill-broadcast/reports-and-transaction#view-transaction-list)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252FhVMKmQrv82DIcdtT9KnW%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D9373584e-339d-445e-bd74-960e38f9237f&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=aae904ef11d26dfaf88640ff4e5b037a&sv=3)

When you send a **Bill Broadcast**, every payment that your customers make will automatically be recorded in your **Report & Transaction** page. This allows you to monitor payment status, track successful and pending bills, and download the full report for reconciliation. For full guidance, visit the official documentation: [**Report & Transaction Docs**](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/manage-reports)

GitBook Assistant

#### **What the Report Looks Like?**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat/bill-broadcast/reports-and-transaction#what-the-report-looks-like)

In the Report & Transaction page, you will see a table containing detailed information for each transaction, including:

GitBook Assistant

*   **Invoice Number**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Transaction Date (GMT+7)**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Amount**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Payment Method** (e.g., DOKU Wallet, Virtual Account)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Status** (Success, Pending, Failed)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Acquirer Name**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Activity** (e.g., Payment, Generate Payment Code)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Additional Info**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   **Export Options** (CSV/XLSX)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

The summary section also displays the total **Successful**, **Pending**, and **Failed** transactions within the selected date range.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Where to Access the Report?**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat/bill-broadcast/reports-and-transaction#where-to-access-the-report)

You can access all Bill Broadcast payment activity through:

GitBook Assistant

👉 **DOKU Dashboard → Report & Transaction** Here you can filter, search, and export the transaction data.

GitBook Assistant

# Cash Out | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out.md)
.

Overview[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out#overview)

------------------------------------------------------------

**Cash Out** is a service that allows authorized users by the merchant to conveniently withdraw cash at convenience stores. Cash Out transaction is made simply by requesting cash withdrawal from the cashier or the POS Machine. Upon requesting cash withdrawal, the cashier or the POS Machine will inquire the users about the specific product type and the name of the merchant. Subsequently, users will be asked for a token, cell phone number, and the desired withdrawal amount. Upon completion of the process, the cashier will dispense the requested cash along with a receipt.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

**Key Features**[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out#key-features)

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Feature

Description

**Easy Access to Cash Withdrawal**

GitBook Assistant

Cash Out provides users with unparalleled accessibility to cash withdrawal services through a vast network of convenience stores. It removes the hassle of locating ATMs or bank branches, offering a reliable alternative for immediate cash needs.

GitBook Assistant

**Supported Convenience Stores**

GitBook Assistant

Users can withdraw cash at over **22,000+ Indomaret stores nationwide**, greatly expanding access to convenient cash withdrawal points.

GitBook Assistant

**Empowering Unbanked Users**

GitBook Assistant

Designed for Indonesians in rural areas with limited banking infrastructure, Cash Out enables cash withdrawals at over **20,000+ convenience store locations**, reaching underserved communities.

GitBook Assistant

**Instant Cash Disbursement**

GitBook Assistant

Upon transaction completion, users receive cash **immediately**, with a receipt provided for record-keeping. This feature is ideal for urgent situations requiring quick access to physical currency.

GitBook Assistant

**Enhanced Security Measures**

GitBook Assistant

The service includes **token-based authentication** and **secure data transmission**, ensuring user transactions and personal information remain confidential and protected.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

**Flow of Transaction**[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out#flow-of-transaction)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Cash Out transaction has the following flow of steps:

GitBook Assistant

1.  **Visit the Nearest Indomaret Store:** Locate the nearest Indomaret store and approach the POS machine or cashier.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  **Inform Cashier:** Request a cash withdrawal from the cashier. The cashier will guide you through the process.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  **Provide Details:** Specify the product type (e.g., DOKU e-Wallet) and name the merchant offering the Cash Out service.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  **Token and Information:** Provide the cashier with the required information, including the token, your cell phone number, and the desired withdrawal amount.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  **Completion:** Once the process is complete, the cashier will dispense the requested cash along with a detailed receipt.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

Requirements[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out#requirements)

--------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Merchant must be a Financial Institution (Bank or non-Bank) that is licensed in Indonesia
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Merchant must have a mobile app to trigger the Cash Out transaction
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Mobile app that belongs to the Merchant must already be live and can be operated by the Merchant’s user
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Merchant must gain approval from the third party (Indomaret).
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

FAQ[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out#faq)

--------------------------------------------------

What is the maximum amount to withdraw with Cash Out?[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out#what-is-the-maximum-amount-to-withdraw-with-cash-out)

For Cash Out with Indomaret, the maximum amount to withdraw is IDR 1,000,000 per transaction. For Cash Out with Alfamart, the maximum amount to withdraw is IDR 5,000,000 per transaction.

GitBook Assistant

How to get started with Cash Out?[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/cash-out#how-to-get-started-with-cash-out)

Please fill and submit [this](https://www.doku.com/en-US/contact-sales)
 form, and our team will contact you for further details.

GitBook Assistant

[PreviousRefund Service](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/refund-service)
[NextEmbedded Wallet](https://docs.doku.com/wallet-as-a-service/embedded-wallet)

Last updated 2 months ago

# Domestic Payouts | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/domestic-payouts.md)
.

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252FCSmK1Mp0zqSuMEwbALbh%252Fimg-localpayouts.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7f4dc6c6-52e9-4adb-81a5-1944dde8bfd8&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=00f0f21a37aaad474dc73aaa340b6bb8&sv=3)

Overview[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/domestic-payouts#overview)

--------------------------------------------------------------------

**Domestic Payouts** is a transfer service by DOKU that allows transfer of funds to all bank accounts and e-Wallets in Indonesia. Submit disbursement instructions through automated APIs or Excel uploads for up to 1,000 transactions per cycle.

GitBook Assistant

Disbursements are swiftly processed, available 24 hours, seven days a week, including weekends and holidays. However, our operation is bound by the banking hours, and funds cannot be disbursed during their inactive periods. Transactions submitted outside these hours will be promptly processed when the banks resume operations.

GitBook Assistant

Domestic Payouts cover 120+ local banks and popular e-Wallets (such as GoPay, OVO, DANA, LinkAja and ShopeePay).

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Key Features[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/domestic-payouts#key-features)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Feature

Description

**Bank Account Validation**

GitBook Assistant

Ensures secure transfers by validating beneficiary bank or e-wallet accounts before initiating payouts.

GitBook Assistant

**Multi-Channel Payouts**

GitBook Assistant

Supports a wide range of payout methods including **bank transfers (Online, BI Fast, SKN/RTGS)** and **e-Wallets**.

GitBook Assistant

**Customizable Workflows**

GitBook Assistant

Allows tailoring of disbursement workflows with custom approval layers and permission settings for secure, compliant processes.

GitBook Assistant

**Real-time Reporting and Tracking**

GitBook Assistant

Offers live insights into transactions, with access to status updates, history, and full reconciliation-ready reports.

GitBook Assistant

**Flexible Fee Structures**

GitBook Assistant

Supports sender-paid, receiver-paid, fee sharing (e.g., 50/50), monthly invoicing, and percentage-based fee models.

GitBook Assistant

**Alert System**

GitBook Assistant

Sends email alerts for key triggers such as balance thresholds (e.g., under IDR 1,000,000) and top-ups, enabling proactive monitoring.

GitBook Assistant

**Multi-Currency Deposits**

GitBook Assistant

Accepts merchant deposits in **IDR, USD, and SGD**, configurable per account, supporting international operations.

GitBook Assistant

**API Integration**

GitBook Assistant

Provides a robust API for seamless system integration, backed by dedicated technical assistance.

GitBook Assistant

**Streamlined PG Settlements**

GitBook Assistant

Enables smooth reconciliation by directing payment gateway settlement funds into payout deposit accounts, allowing refunds and transfers to agents, vendors, or partners.

GitBook Assistant

Our operations are subject to banking hours, and funds cannot be disbursed during non-operational periods.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

FAQ[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/domestic-payouts#faq)

----------------------------------------------------------

What are the supported banks for Domestic Payouts?[](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/domestic-payouts#what-are-the-supported-banks-for-domestic-payouts)

You may visit this [List of Banks](https://docs.doku.com/payouts/domestic-payouts/list-of-banks)
 article for the full list of all supported banks.

 form, and our team will contact you for further details.

# DOKU e-Wallet | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet.md)
.

Overview[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#overview)

---------------------------------------------------------------------

DOKU e-Wallet is a digital wallet service that enables users to make online and offline payments safely, anytime and anywhere. You can use DOKU e-Wallet to pay various bills and subscription-based purchases, top up and withdraw cash, as well as transfer funds to other DOKU e-Wallet users.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Key Features[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#key-features)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------

#### **Mobile Wallet Management**[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#mobile-wallet-management)

DOKU e-Wallet serves as a centralized hub for users to manage their digital wallets effortlessly. Users can easily check their wallet balance, review transaction histories, and monitor their financial activities in real-time. This feature empowers individuals to maintain control over their digital funds with convenience and accessibility.

GitBook Assistant

#### Premium Account[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#premium-account)

While the Regular Account only allows a maximum balance of IDR 2,000,000, a Premium Account allows a maximum balance of IDR 20,000,000. You are also enabled to transfer balance to other DOKU e-Wallet users and withdraw balance via Bank Transfer.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Online and Offline Payments**[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#online-and-offline-payments)

One of the primary functionalities of DOKU e-Wallet is the ability to make secure and seamless online and offline payments. Users can shop for a wide array of goods and services, ranging from e-commerce purchases to digital subscriptions, using their mobile devices. DOKU e-Wallet provides a user-friendly interface, ensuring a smooth and efficient payment process for various online transactions.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Bill Payments**[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#bill-payments)

DOKU e-Wallet often supports bill payments for utilities, telecommunications, and other services. Users can settle their bills directly through the app, eliminating the need for traditional payment methods and providing a more efficient and time-saving alternative. DOKU e-Wallet supports the following list of Bill Payments:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Mobile Balance Topup (_Pulsa_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Mobile Package Data (_Paket Data_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  PLN Prepaid/Postpaid (_Prabayar/Pascabayar_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  PDAM
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Internet & Cable TV
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
6.  Telkom
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
7.  Phone Bill (_Telfon Pascabayar_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
8.  BPJS (_Badan Penyelenggara Jaminan Sosial_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
9.  Tax & Non-Tax Payments (_Penerimaan Negara_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
10.  Cash Withdrawal (_Tarik Tunai_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
11.  On-Store Payment (_Belanja di Toko_)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

#### QRIS Payments[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#qris-payments)

QRIS (Quick Response Code Indonesian Standard) is a standardized QR code system used for digital payments in Indonesia. DOKU e-Wallet allows users to make payments by scanning QR codes using their mobile devices. The QRIS system is designed to facilitate interoperability among different payment service providers, making it easier for consumers and merchants to transact across various platforms.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Fund Transfer to Banks and e-Wallets**[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#fund-transfer-to-banks-and-e-wallets)

DOKU e-Wallet facilitates quick and secure money transfers, allowing users to send funds to other DOKU users or individuals who have DOKU accounts. This feature is particularly useful for peer-to-peer transactions, splitting bills, or sending money to friends and family members, all within the convenience of the mobile application.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Promotions and Rewards**[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#promotions-and-rewards)

To enhance user engagement, DOKU e-Wallet may offer promotions, discounts, and rewards programs. Users may receive exclusive deals or cashback rewards for using the app for specific transactions or shopping with partner merchants, providing added incentives for continued usage.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Security Features**[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#security-features)

Recognizing the importance of security in financial transactions, DOKU e-Wallet incorporates robust security measures. These may include encryption protocols, multi-factor authentication, and other security features to safeguard users' financial information and transactions, instilling confidence in the use of the digital wallet.

GitBook Assistant

#### **Merchant Integration**[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#merchant-integration)

DOKU e-Wallet seamlessly integrates with a diverse range of online and offline merchants. This integration enables users to make purchases from a variety of businesses, including retail stores, restaurants, and service providers. By connecting with merchants, the app enhances the overall shopping experience, offering users the flexibility to pay for goods and services using their DOKU digital wallet.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Installation[](https://docs.doku.com/mobile-apps/doku-e-wallet#installation)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------

DOKU e-Wallet supports both iOS and Android operating system. Visit the links below to download and install DOKU e-Wallet based on the operating system that your phone supports:

GitBook Assistant

1.  DOKU e-Wallet on [App Store](https://apps.apple.com/id/app/doku-e-wallet/id1169936140)
     (iOS)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  DOKU e-Wallet on [Play Store](https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.dokuwallet.android&hl=en&gl=US)
     (Android)
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

-----------------------------------------------------------

. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/no-integration-products/payment-link/customize-checkout-page.md)
.

Sort Payment Method[](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/no-integration-products/payment-link/customize-checkout-page#sort-payment-method)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You can sort as well as show/hide payment methods that you have activated for your Business Account. You may do so by following these simple steps:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Log in to [DOKU Dashboard](https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/login?utm_source=docs)
    , and then access the side navigation bar
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Select **Settings** from the menu
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  **Settings** page will appear. Under **Accept Payments** section, select **Checkout Appearance**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  **Checkout Page Configuration** page will appear, then click **Payment Method Settings** tab where you can sort the payment methods by dragging the payment method to the desired order, and show/hide the payment methods by ticking or unticking the payment method
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Click **Save** button to save your configuration
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

Customize Interface[](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/no-integration-products/payment-link/customize-checkout-page#customize-interface)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  Log in to [DOKU Dashboard](https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/login?utm_source=docs)
    , and then access the side navigation bar
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  Select **Settings** from the menu
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  **Settings** page will appear. Under **Accept Payments** section, select **Checkout Appearance**
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  **Checkout Page Configuration** page will appear, then click **Interface Settings** tab where you will find several options for customizing the look and feel of your checkout page, including the ability to add your logo and change the background color that fits your brand
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Click **Save** button to save your configuration
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

* * *

FAQ[](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/no-integration-products/payment-link/customize-checkout-page#faq)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Why is my payment method missing on the checkout page?[](https://docs.doku.com/accept-payments/no-integration-products/payment-link/customize-checkout-page#why-is-my-payment-method-missing-on-the-checkout-page)

A payment method may not appear on the checkout page if:

GitBook Assistant

*   It has not been activated for your account, or
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Its status is still inactive.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

Please refer to the activation guide on [Manage Payment Methods](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/manage-payment-methods)
 to check and confirm whether your payment method is active.

GitBook Assistant

# PayChat | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat.md)
.

Availability: 🇮🇩 Indonesian Business Account only

GitBook Assistant

**PayChat** is an all-in-one WhatsApp-based solution that helps merchants sell, communicate, and collect payments—all within a single platform. Designed to simplify digital transactions, PayChat combines messaging and payment in one seamless experience.

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252Ftyq36ei2ppt0gK4kDiMP%252F21102025_EDM-PayChat.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D7652ca2a-38c2-4a4d-93e9-f935386dc63b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=9f104357075fbee775a40f6cc25497f9&sv=3)

PayChat is an all-in-one WhatsApp-based solution that helps merchants sell, communicate, and collect payments—all within a single platform.

GitBook Assistant

**Overview**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#overview)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### **WhatsApp Business**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#whatsapp-business)

Create and manage your official WhatsApp Business account through PayChat.

GitBook Assistant

*   Build a professional business profile
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Connect your own number
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Send broadcasts, receive orders, and manage all conversations
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Fully integrated in one unified platform
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

**One WhatsApp number, a more professional customer experience.** 👉 [_Learn more_](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat/whatsapp-business-waba)

GitBook Assistant

### **Accept Order**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#accept-order)

Receive and manage customer orders directly through WhatsApp—without external forms.

GitBook Assistant

*   Customers view products and prices inside the chat
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Orders arrive in real time
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Payment happens right after confirmation
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Ideal for events, pre-orders, and daily sales
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

**A faster, cleaner, frictionless sales process.** 👉 [_Learn more_](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat/accept-order)

GitBook Assistant

### **Bill Broadcast**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#bill-broadcast)

Send messages to multiple customers at once—fast, personal, and efficient.

GitBook Assistant

*   Share promotions, announcements, or payment reminders
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Available via dashboard or API
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Uses official WhatsApp channels
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
*   Higher reach and better engagement
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

**No complex system—just upload and send.** 👉 [_Learn more_](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat/bill-broadcast)

GitBook Assistant

* * *

**Benefits**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#benefits)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#### 💬 **One WhatsApp for All Your Business Needs**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#one-whatsapp-for-all-your-business-needs)

Manage orders, transactions, and customer interactions without switching platforms. Customers stay on WhatsApp, and you stay focused on selling.

GitBook Assistant

#### ⚡ **Faster, Smoother Payments**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#faster-smoother-payments)

PayChat combines conversations and payments into a single flow. Customers simply chat, choose, and pay—right inside WhatsApp.

GitBook Assistant

#### 🎯 **More Effective Communication & Promotions**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#more-effective-communication-and-promotions)

Send broadcast messages, payment reminders, or promotional campaigns in seconds. Reach customers through the channel they use every day.

GitBook Assistant

#### 🧩 **No Integration Needed**[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#no-integration-needed)

PayChat is ready to use out of the box. Activate the features, connect your WhatsApp number, and start selling instantly.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

FAQ[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#faq)

------------------------------------------------------------------

Do I need a website or app to use PayChat?[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#do-i-need-a-website-or-app-to-use-paychat)

No. PayChat is designed to work without any integration. All features—Accept Order, Broadcast Message, and My WhatsApp Business—can be activated and used instantly. However to verify your WhatsApp Business you need to have a website for requirement.

GitBook Assistant

Is PayChat safe for handling payments?[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#is-paychat-safe-for-handling-payments)

Yes. PayChat is powered by DOKU, a trusted payment gateway in Indonesia. All transactions follow strict security standards to ensure safe and seamless payments.

GitBook Assistant

Do customers need to install anything?[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#do-customers-need-to-install-anything)

No. Customers only need WhatsApp. All interactions—ordering, receiving messages, and completing payments—happen within the WhatsApp app they already use.

GitBook Assistant

How do I start using PayChat?[](https://docs.doku.com/subscription-and-billing/paychat#how-do-i-start-using-paychat)

Simply sign in to your DOKU Dashboard, activate PayChat, connect your WhatsApp number, and start using the features instantly. No technical setup required.

GitBook Assistant

# Create Account | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/create-account.md)
.

Create a Business Account[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/create-account#create-a-business-account)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

In this section, you will learn how to become a DOKU merchant by creating your first DOKU Business Account. The following is a step-by-step guide on how you could create a Business Account:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Visit DOKU Dashboard Registration page
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
    *   Indonesia and other countries: [https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/register](https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/register)
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    *   Malaysia: [https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/register?country=MY](https://dashboard.doku.com/bo/register?country=MY)
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    
2.  Fill the registration form by entering your full name, business name, business email address, phone number, and password
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Review and agree to the **Terms and Conditions** and **Privacy Policy**, then submit the form
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  Verify your account by entering the OTP that was sent to your email address
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
5.  Once your OTP is authenticated, your Business Account will be successfully created. You can proceed to activate your Business account by following the next guide [here](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/activate-business)
    .
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

If you have an existing Business Account, you can skip this process by creating a User Account. [Learn more](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/manage-team-members)

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252FQ9frMyGYdwJwEIR7Isc6%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D495a8537-0aab-4f13-b1b6-a030a25a2037&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=eae3ea9d99cfb877b2be463a0f2cf5ca&sv=3)

DOKU Dashboard Registration Page

* * *

Create a Sandbox Account[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/create-account#create-a-sandbox-account)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

While DOKU Business Account is used to accept real payments in the live environment, DOKU Sandbox Account is a demo account that you can use to simulate payments in the testing environment. If you are looking to test payments and explore our services and products, the following is a step-by-step guide on how you could create a DOKU Sandbox Account:

GitBook Assistant

1.  Visit DOKU Sandbox Registration page
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
    *   Indonesia and other countries: [https://sandbox.doku.com/bo/sandbox-registration](https://sandbox.doku.com/bo/sandbox-registration)
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    *   Malaysia: [https://sandbox.doku.com/bo/sandbox-registration?country=MY](https://sandbox.doku.com/bo/sandbox-registration?country=MY)
        
        GitBook Assistant
        
    
2.  Fill the registration form by entering your full name, business name, business email address, and password
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
3.  Review and agree to the **Terms and Conditions** and **Privacy Policy**, then submit the form
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
4.  DOKU Sandbox Account has been successfully created. You can proceed to log in to [DOKU Sandbox](https://sandbox.doku.com/bo/login?utm_source=docs)
     using the credentials you provided in the registration form.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252F4slaR1UcYA84QrTnBmSP%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D300cdb7c-9a51-434e-a60c-dc88caeb767e&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5f9c901c161fdaeadfb510720893fc19&sv=3)

DOKU Sandbox Registration Page

* * *

FAQ[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/create-account#faq)

------------------------------------------------------------

I did not receive any OTP during registration. What should I do?[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/create-account#i-did-not-receive-any-otp-during-registration.-what-should-i-do)

You can troubleshoot this issue in two ways:

GitBook Assistant

1.  The OTP may not have been successfully sent due to an invalid email address entered during registration. Please ensure that the email address you provided is correct and capable of receiving emails from outside your organization.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    
2.  The email might be inside your Spam folder. Please ensure that you have checked your Spam folder.
    
    GitBook Assistant
    

What is the difference between a User Account and a Business Account?[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/create-account#what-is-the-difference-between-a-user-account-and-a-business-account)

A **Business Account** represents your company as a whole, while a **User Account** represents an individual member within your company. A single Business Account can have multiple User Accounts, depending on how many members are needed to manage your business operations.

GitBook Assistant

Can I sign up for multiple Business Accounts with the same company and user data?[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/create-account#can-i-sign-up-for-multiple-business-accounts-with-the-same-company-and-user-data)

Yes, although this is not recommended, as our risk screening team may not approve your account verification. If your company operates multiple lines of business and you wish to accept payments for each, you can register a single Business Account and enable the Multi-brand feature. You can learn more about Multi-brand on [Manage Multiple Brands](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/manage-multiple-brands)
.

GitBook Assistant

If your questions cannot be found here, please visit [General FAQs](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/contact-support#general-faqs)
 for further information.

GitBook Assistant

[NextActivate Business](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/activate-business)

Last updated 7 months ago

# Resubmit Rejected Data | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/resubmit-rejected-data.md)
.

### Rejection Notifications[](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/resubmit-rejected-data#rejection-notifications)

Upon any data rejection, you will receive an email notifying which data is being rejected. You can click on "Update Data" or the Pencil Icon as shown below to be redirected to where you should update the rejected data.

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252F6rsuS3B7ZAgBKmMS0TEg%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc8292d29-c8fe-4066-a105-b5be2f3a83da&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b61468c7b73651bab3f73094dcdac088&sv=3)

Besides email, we will also notify you in your homepage and via notification center. Simply click on "Update →" to be redirected to the Business Info page where you can update your rejected data.

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252FKsofgXxglBv1h41vJDxb%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dfcbed94e-d584-4afe-aebf-c9b9e1a88937&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=c809245f45b3c2e539346ead2f06226f&sv=3)

Once you are redirected, you will notice that we have highlighted which tab you should update your data in via the ⚠️ icon. You can also observe the reason for the rejection based on the reason stated. Simply click on "Edit Data" and submit it once you've made your changes. Our Team will then review and verify it!

GitBook Assistant

![](https://docs.doku.com/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1457116080-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FWIsxU2UGMS7iidoMSuyz%252Fuploads%252F3xocdgzYl4TdLpcSlFfR%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dba2ee782-e50f-4e25-be07-dec31b4d7c77&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=44b7a4af0bff48b1df9016d7fcad5137&sv=3)

[NextManage Team Members](https://docs.doku.com/get-started/manage-business/manage-team-members)

# Glossary | DOKU Docs

[🇲🇾 senangPay Merchant? Need help using the DOKU Dashboard?\
\
Visit the support guide here](https://guide.senangpay.com/)

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.doku.com/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary.md)
.

A.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#a)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Aggregator Settlement** Aggregator Settlement refers to a settlement type where transaction funds that are received by the bank or payment channel are settled by DOKU to merchant. The settlement period for Aggregator Settlement follows the settlement schedule by DOKU. Aggregator Settlement allows DOKU to charge by deducting fees from the settlement.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

B.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#b)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Bank Transfer** Bank Transfer is a payment method provided by DOKU that facilitates payment by enabling customers to transfer funds directly from their bank accounts to a virtual account. This payment can be done via a mobile banking app, web banking, or an ATM.

GitBook Assistant

**BIN (Bank Identification Number)** BIN is a unique identifier generated by the bank or payment channel that is assigned to merchants. This ID is usually used for payment methods such as Bank Transfer and Convenience Store.

GitBook Assistant

**Brand ID** Brand ID or Client ID is an identification number for your brand account. Client ID is used for authentication purposes in the context of technical integration and reporting an issue to DOKU Care.

GitBook Assistant

**Business Account** Business ID is a distinctive identifier assigned to the company account of the merchant. One business account may have more than one brand account.

GitBook Assistant

**Business ID** Brand ID or Client ID is a distinctive identifier assigned to the company account of the merchant.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

C.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#c)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Callback URL** Callback URL is a web address specified by merchants to receive notifications and updates about transaction statuses. In the context of the payment service provider, it is utilized to inform merchants about successful payments, chargebacks, and other relevant events.

GitBook Assistant

**Cash Out** Cash Out refers to the capability provided by the payment service provider for users to convert their electronic funds into physical currency. This feature enhances flexibility for users who prefer or require cash transactions.

GitBook Assistant

**Chargeback** Chargeback occurs when a customer disputes a transaction and the funds are returned to the customer. The payment service provider manages the chargeback process, ensuring fairness and resolution between merchants and customers.

GitBook Assistant

**Client ID** Client ID or Brand ID is an identification number for your brand account. Client ID is used for authentication purposes in the context of technical integration and reporting an issue to DOKU Care.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

D.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#d)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Digital Catalog** Digital Catalog is a product provided by DOKU that allows merchants to create an online catalog that showcases a comprehensive overview of their products or services available for purchase.

GitBook Assistant

**Direct API** Direct API is an integration method provided by DOKU, where it allows merchants to create customized payment flows and integrate them directly into their websites while adhering to security and compliance standards.

GitBook Assistant

**Direct Settlement** Direct Settlement refers to a settlement type where transaction funds that are received by the bank or payment channel are settled directly to the merchant. This expedites the settlement process for merchants.

GitBook Assistant

**DIPC (Direct Inquiry Payment Code)** DIPC is a method of generating a payment code where merchants first register the payment code, and DOKU will forward the inquiry request to merchants when a customer chooses to make a payment. This is suitable for a top-up payment, especially for static Virtual Account.

GitBook Assistant

**DGPC (DOKU Generated Payment Code)** DGPC is a method of generating a payment code where merchants send a payment request to DOKU, and DOKU will create the unique payment code and send back it to merchants. This is suitable for an e-commerce business model.

GitBook Assistant

**DOKU e-Wallet** DOKU e-Wallet is a digital wallet provided by DOKU, allowing users to store funds and make electronic transactions securely.

GitBook Assistant

**DOKU Care** DOKU Care refers to the customer support and assistance services provided by DOKU to address user inquiries, issues, and concerns.

GitBook Assistant

**DOKU Checkout** DOKU Checkout is an integration method provided by DOKU where the checkout page is hosted by DOKU, and merchants are not required to have their own checkout page.

GitBook Assistant

**Domestic Payouts** Domestic Payouts is a service provided by DOKU that involves the distribution of funds within the same country, enhancing the efficiency of financial transactions for merchants.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

E.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#e)

-----------------------------------------------------

**e-Wallet** e-Wallet is is a payment method provided by DOKU that allows payment acceptance by deducting balance of Indonesian electronic wallets.

GitBook Assistant

**Embedded Wallet** Embedded Wallet is a product provided by DOKU where a wallet is integrated directly into a platform or application, offering users a seamless and unified payment experience.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

G.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#g)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Global Payouts** Global Payouts is a service provided by DOKU that enables distribution of funds across international borders, catering to the needs of businesses and merchants with a global presence.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

J.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#j)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Juragan DOKU** Juragan DOKU is a mobile app that enables small-to-medium businesses to accept payments both online and offline by generating payment links or showcasing your products through a catalog.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

K.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#k)

-----------------------------------------------------

**KKI (Kartu Kredit Indonesia)** Kartu Kredit Indonesia a payment method provided by DOKU that enabling customers to accept payments with GPN cards.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

M.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#m)

-----------------------------------------------------

**MID (Merchant ID)** Merchant ID is a unique identifier generated by the bank or payment channel that is assigned to merchants. It facilitates the tracking and management of transactions associated with specific merchants. This ID is usually used for payment methods such as Cards, Direct Debit, e-Wallets, and PayLater.

GitBook Assistant

**MGPC (Merchant Generated Payment Code)** MGPC is a method of generating a payment code where merchants can generate their own payment code and forwards the payment code to DOKU. This is suitable for a top-up payment.

GitBook Assistant

**Multi-brand** Multi-brand is a feature provided by DOKU that allows merchants to have multiple brand accounts in one business account.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

N.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#n)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Notification URL** Notification URL is a web address designated to receive real-time notifications about transaction events, enabling merchants to stay informed about payment activities.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

P.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#p)

-----------------------------------------------------

**PayLater** PayLater is a payment method provided by DOKU that allows customers to complete their purchase without having to pay immediately; they are typically given a certain period of time, often interest-free, to settle the payment via a third-party financing company.

GitBook Assistant

**Payment Link** Payment Link is a unique URL generated by the payment service provider, enabling users to make payments by clicking on the link. It is often used for online invoices and transactions.

GitBook Assistant

**Payment Method/Payment Channel** Payment Method or Payment Channel refers to the route through which a customer can make a payment such as Virtual Account BCA, OVO, and Akulaku.

GitBook Assistant

**Payment Type** Payment Type refers to the various ways users can make payments such as e-Wallets, PayLater, and Convenience Store.

GitBook Assistant

**Promo Engine** Promo Engine is a system that manages and applies promotional offers and discounts within the payment service provider's platform.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

Q.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#q)

-----------------------------------------------------

**QRIS** QRIS (Quick Response Code Indonesian Standard) is a payment method provided by DOKU that enabling customers to scan a QR code to make payments via mobile banking apps and e-Wallets.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

S.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#s)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Sandbox (DOKU Sandbox)** A Sandbox is a testing environment provided by DOKU for developers to experiment and ensure the functionality of their applications or integrations in a controlled setting.

GitBook Assistant

**Secret Key** Secret Key is a confidential code that is used for technical integration with DOKU APIs.

GitBook Assistant

**Settlement** Settlement is a process where the payment gateway (DOKU) transfers the funds that were received for transactions that have been processed by the merchant during a certain period of time. The settlement period varies for every payment method as well as the settlement scheme (aggregator or direct).

GitBook Assistant

**SNAP (Standar Nasional Open API Pembayaran)** SNAP is the National Open API Payment Standard established by Bank Indonesia to create a healthy, competitive and innovative payment system industry; encouraging integration, interconnection, interoperability, as well as security and reliability of payment system infrastructure; and/or improve healthy, efficient and fair market practices in the implementation of the payment system.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

U.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#u)

-----------------------------------------------------

**User Account** A User Account is a personal account that is created by individuals within one Business Account. One business account may have more than one user account.

GitBook Assistant

* * *

V.[](https://docs.doku.com/miscellaneous/glossary#v)

-----------------------------------------------------

**Virtual Account Close Amount** Virtual Account Close Amount only allows payment of an amount that has already been set by the merchant. It is usually used used in e-commerce payments, where they can easily identify when the full payment has been made as the transactions are only completed when the exact amount is transferred into the virtual account for that particular transaction.

GitBook Assistant

**Virtual Account Dynamic** Virtual Account Dynamic is a type of Virtual Account where the virtual account number is randomly assigned. This is typically used for one off payments on e-commerce sites.

GitBook Assistant

**Virtual Account Open Amount** Virtual Account Open Amount allows the payment to be received with any amount and is set by the user immediately. It is usually used for top-up cases.

GitBook Assistant

**Virtual Account Static** Virtual Account Static is a type of Virtual Account where the virtual account number is uniquely defined for each user. The virtual account number generally uses the user’s mobile phone number as a fixed virtual account number that makes multiple top-ups easier.

GitBook Assistant

**Virtual Terminal** Virtual Terminal is a web-based interface provided by DOKU, allowing merchants to manually enter and process credit card transactions without the need for physical card terminals. Virtual terminals are commonly used for phone orders, mail orders, or in situations where a card is not present for swiping or tapping.

GitBook Assistant

* * *
